We introduce Visual Attribution Distillation (VAD), a counterfactual target-reconstruction algorithm that estimates the visually attributable part of a teacher correction. At each student-generated prefix, VAD evaluates the same fixed teacher with the relevant evidence present and removed. The corresponding change in centered log-probabilities defines ut, a signed proxy for the visual evidence direction that estimates how revealing the evidence supports or refutes candidate tokens. VAD projects the original correction onto this proxy to obtain an intervention-aligned component and a proxy-unexplained residual, then reconstructs a student-anchored target from the former. During training, this reconstructed target supplies the primary supervision signal, while the privileged teacher contributes a weak regularizer. Across six fine-grained visual benchmarks at 4B and 9B scales, VAD outperforms direct privileged-view distillation and visual-advantage weighting. Token- level and controlled-target analyses show that the proxy-aligned component is enriched in task-relevant visual corrections and yields stronger target shifts, especially when evidence refutes a mistaken answer. These results support counterfactual target reconstruction as an effective alternative to source-mixed supervision.

</details>


### [103] [Beacon: Knowing When and How to Perform Agentic Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28595)
*Qixun Wang,Yang Shi,Letian Cheng,Zhuoran Zhang,Yan He,Yuqi Tang,Qi Zhang,Xinlei Yu,Ruizhe Chen,Tianrun Xu,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Haotian Wang,Xianghua Ying*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Beacon模型，通过必要性感知自适应奖励和提示引导能力扩展两种强化学习机制，解决智能体视觉推理中工具调用的模式自适应性和工具效果两大问题，在多个基准上取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体视觉推理模型在工具使用上存在两个关键缺陷：一方面模式自适应性不足，模型无法判断何时真正需要工具而盲目调用，造成不必要的计算开销；另一方面工具效果不佳，工具带来的收益在困难样本上被简单样本中引入的错误所抵消。作者希望通过系统分析这两大维度来推动该领域的改进。

Method: 提出Beacon模型，核心包含两个强化学习阶段的机制：(1) Necessity-Aware Adaptive Reward——基于任务必要性鼓励模型自适应地调用工具；(2) Hint-Guided Capability Expansion——通过提示引导增强模型在最具挑战性问题上的工具使用能力。

Result: 在多个多样化基准上的大量实验表明，Beacon在整体性能上表现优异，同时在模式自适应性（MA）和工具效果（TE）两个维度上都取得了实质性提升。

Conclusion: 智能体视觉推理应该以提升任务成功率为根本目标，Beacon通过机制设计同时优化模式自适应性和工具效果，证明这两者可以兼顾，为多模态大模型的智能体视觉推理提供了新的思路。

Abstract: The fundamental goal of agentic visual reasoning is to improve the success rate of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) on complex tasks, rather than merely equipping them with a sophisticated yet inefficient reasoning paradigm. In this work, we rethink agentic visual reasoning through two key dimensions of tool use: Mode Adaptiveness (MA) and Tool Effect (TE). Mode Adaptiveness characterizes whether an MLLM can recognize when tools are truly necessary and invoke them accordingly, thereby avoiding unnecessary computational overhead while improving performance on challenging problems that require tool assistance. Tool Effect characterizes the actual impact of tool use: tools should extend the model's capabilities on problems unsolvable through text-only reasoning, while avoiding additional errors on problems that the model can already solve without tools. We conduct a comprehensive analysis to quantify these two properties and empirically reveal that existing agentic visual reasoning models exhibit limited Mode Adaptiveness, while the gains produced by tool use on hard examples are largely offset by the harm introduced on easy examples that the models can already solve. Motivated by these observations, we propose Beacon, a novel agentic visual reasoning model that achieves stronger overall performance, improved Mode Adaptiveness, and genuine tool-induced performance gains. At the core of Beacon are the Necessity-Aware Adaptive Reward and the Hint-Guided Capability Expansion mechanism in the reinforcement learning stage, which respectively encourage adaptive tool invocation based on task necessity and strengthen the model's tool-use capability on the most challenging problems. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate the strong overall performance of Beacon and its substantial improvements in both Mode Adaptiveness and Tool Effect.

</details>


### [104] [Chimera: Designing and Chinchilla-Scaling Hybrid Visual Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28611)
*Chongjian Ge,Hanwen Jiang,Tianyu Wang,Jiuxiang Gu,Yiran Xu,Ziwen Chen,Shaoteng Liu,Jing Shi,Yicong Hong,Zefan Cai,Hailin Jin,Hao Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Chimera，一种混合视觉扩散骨干网络，结合Kimi Delta Attention（线复杂度）、多潜头注意力（全局交互）和模态感知短卷积（局部时空信息），并配备稀疏MoE层。通过HeteroP模块级超参数迁移方案拟合Chinchilla式计算最优法则，训练出11B参数（2B激活）模型，在计算效率上达到全注意力基线的7.3倍，且能零样本从5秒训练片段外推至30秒视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率图像、长视频和多模态上下文使得全注意力的二次方计算成本变得不可承受，亟需一种高效且可扩展的视觉扩散架构来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: Chimera架构将文本、图像和视频token以栅格顺序统一处理（无需位置嵌入），核心组件包括：KDA（线性复杂度长上下文状态追踪）、MLA（全局直接交互）、模态感知短卷积（局部时空上下文）和稀疏MoE层（扩展容量同时控制激活计算）。此外提出HeteroP模块级超参数迁移方案，依据各张量的函数扇入和模型深度跨宽度/深度迁移超参数，拟合Chinchilla式计算最优法则以指导模型和训练数据规模选择。

Result: 三个核心结果：（1）稠密骨干在预训练扩散损失上比全注意力Wan-2.1 2B基线计算高效1.7倍，完整系统达7.3倍；（2）无需长度特化微调，从5秒训练片段零样本外推至30秒视频，最后5秒仅6.5% FID退化；（3）拟合法则显示图像预训练中计算在激活模型大小与训练token数间近乎均匀分配，视频预训练在高预算下略偏向模型大小。

Conclusion: Chimera与HeteroP为设计和大规模扩展高效长上下文扩散架构提供了系统性基础，验证了混合注意力+MoE+计算最优法则组合的有效性。

Abstract: Visual generation increasingly requires high-resolution images, long videos, and multimodal context, making the quadratic cost of full attention prohibitive. We introduce Chimera, a hybrid visual diffusion backbone with a principled scaling recipe. Chimera processes text, image, and video tokens in one raster-ordered stream without positional embeddings. It combines Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) for long-context state tracking with O(N) complexity, interleaved Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) for direct global interaction, and modality-aware short convolutions for local spatiotemporal context. Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers expand capacity while controlling activated compute. To scale this heterogeneous architecture, we introduce HeteroP, a module-wise scheme that transfers hyperparameters across width and depth according to each tensor's functional fan-in and model depth. HeteroP yields a consistently tuned family used to fit Chinchilla-style compute-optimal laws for activated model size, training-token count, and image-video data ratio. Guided by these laws, we train an 11B-parameter Chimera with 2B activated parameters. Experiments show three results. First, measured by pretraining diffusion loss, the dense backbone is 1.7x as compute-efficient as a matched full-attention Wan-2.1 2B baseline, while the complete system reaches 7.3x. Second, without length-specific fine-tuning, Chimera extrapolates zero-shot from 5-second training clips to 30-second videos, with only 6.5% FID degradation in the last five seconds. Third, the fitted laws show that compute-optimal image pretraining divides compute nearly evenly between activated model size and training-token count, whereas video pretraining modestly favors model size at higher budgets. These results establish a foundation for designing and scaling efficient long-context diffusion architectures.

</details>


### [105] [PhiZero: A World Model Built Around Physical Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28624)
*Shuyao Shang,Yuqi Wang,Ruopeng Gao,Xu Chen,Tieniu Tan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了PhiZero，一种基于物理语言（physical language）的物理世界模型。物理语言是一种紧凑的离散世界状态转换表示，PhiZero采用"先推理后渲染"（reason-then-render）的范式：先以物理语言序列推断未来世界演化，再将推理结果渲染为视频。实验表明其在生成与理解基准上能建模物理一致的世界演化，并展现出在交互式世界建模、细粒度动作条件模拟和零样本运动迁移方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理世界模型直接在像素空间中预测未来视频，导致底层世界动力学隐含在高维视觉预测器中，缺乏显式推理能力。人类能够从视觉经验中抽象预测结构并以自然语言组织进行显式推理，受此启发，作者希望构建一种基于显式物理语言的物理世界模型。

Method: PhiZero从野外视频中通过自监督学习物理语言（一种紧凑的离散世界状态转换表示），采用reason-then-render范式：首先以物理语言序列显式推断未来世界演化，然后将推断出的状态转换渲染为视频。

Result: 在生成与理解基准上的广泛实验验证了PhiZero建模物理一致世界演化的能力，并展示了其在现实交互式世界建模、细粒度动作条件模拟和零样本运动迁移方面的潜力。

Conclusion: PhiZero证明了以显式离散物理语言作为中间表示，能够有效实现物理一致的世界演化建模，并支持多种下游应用，为物理世界模型的显式推理提供了新方向。

Abstract: We introduce PhiZero, a physical world model built around physical language, a compact discrete representation of world-state transitions. Existing physical world models typically predict future videos directly in pixel space, leaving the underlying world dynamics implicit within high-dimensional visual predictors. Motivated by humans' ability to abstract predictive structure from visual experience and organize it in natural language for explicit reasoning, we learn physical language from in-the-wild videos through self-supervision and use it to explicitly reason about how the physical world evolves. Accordingly, PhiZero adopts a reason-then-render paradigm: it first infers future world evolution as a physical-language sequence and then renders the inferred transitions into videos. Extensive experiments across generation and understanding benchmarks validate the ability of PhiZero to model physically coherent world evolution. We further show its potential for realistic and interactive world modeling, fine-grained action-conditioned simulation, and zero-shot motion transfer.

</details>


### [106] [ACE-Data-0: Human-Centric Ambient Capture as Embodied Data Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28625)
*Yukang Cao,Haozhe Xie,Beichen Wen,Runmao Yao,Yinghao Liu,Yue Huang,Zhichao Liao,Yunxiang Wang,Haiheng Liu,Xingshun Tian,Dawei Su,Long Zhuo,Dacheng Tao,Xiaogang Wang,Liang Pan,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了环境捕获引擎（ACE），一个人类为中心的数据引擎，将真实家庭环境转化为空间校准、时间同步的录制工作室，在桌面尺度和房间尺度同时捕获多模态数据，构建了包含150小时、1700万帧、75000个交互片段的大规模数据集ACE-Data-0，并提出了层级基准以评估具身智能模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能面临基本的数据瓶颈：现有数据集在视角、模态或空间尺度上碎片化，无法完整捕捉第一人称感知、全身运动、灵巧操作、物体状态、声音和触觉随人类目标追求而共同演化的完整感知-行动回路。

Method: ACE在两种互补尺度上运行：桌面尺度解决手-物精细操作，房间尺度捕获全身运动、移动和家居互动。记录包括第一人称和多视角第三人称视频、全身和关节手部运动、物体几何与6-DoF轨迹、音频和触觉信号。指令采用目标级而非步骤级，保留自然行为变化。此外构建了从信号到场景组件再到交互的层级基准。

Result: ACE-Data-0包含150小时和1700万视频帧，覆盖200个任务类别，由50名参与者在2个环境中完成共75000个交互片段。评估最先进方法揭示了在接触、遮挡、自我运动和长时间跨度下的显著差距。

Conclusion: ACE-Data-0提供了同步的人类演示，包含对齐的感知、运动和接触监督，为模仿学习、世界模型、视觉-语言-行动系统和具身AI提供了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence faces a fundamental data bottleneck. Models must capture how first-person perception, whole-body motion, dexterous manipulation, object state, sound, and touch evolve together as humans pursue goals over time. Existing datasets fragment this experience across viewpoints, modalities, or spatial scales, leaving the full perception-action loop only partially observed. We introduce the Ambient Capture Engine (ACE), a human-centric data engine that transforms real home environments into spatially calibrated, temporally synchronized recording studios. ACE operates at two complementary scales: a table-scale configuration resolves hand-object manipulation, while a room-scale configuration captures whole-body motion, locomotion, and interactions across a furnished home. ACE records egocentric and multi-view exocentric video, full-body and articulated hand motion, object geometry and 6-DoF trajectories, audio, and tactile signals as a unified multisensory stream. Using ACE, we build ACE-Data-0, comprising 150 hours and 17M video frames across 200 task categories, performed by 50 participants in 2 environments, for a total of 75,000 interaction episodes. The dataset spans atomic manipulation, long-horizon chains of household activities, and human-scene interaction, while preserving natural behavioral variation through goal-level rather than step-by-step instructions. We further introduce a hierarchical benchmark that progresses from signals to scene components and then to interactions. Evaluations of state-of-the-art methods expose substantial gaps under contact, occlusion, egomotion, and long temporal horizons. ACE-Data-0 provides synchronized human demonstrations with aligned perceptual, kinematic, and contact supervision, offering a scalable foundation for imitation learning, world models, vision-language-action systems, and embodied AI.

</details>


### [107] [ReToken: One Token to Improve Vision-Language Models for Visual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28627)
*Yao Xiao,Reuben Tan,Zhen Zhu,Yuqun Wu,Jianfeng Gao,Derek Hoiem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReToken提出了一种轻量级可学习嵌入，作为显式检索目标从预填充的视觉KV缓存中选择与查询相关的稀疏视觉token，在图像和视频基准上均取得显著性能提升，且训练和推理均可单卡完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视觉上下文对视觉语言模型构成挑战：随着干扰物数量增加性能退化，同时GPU内存限制下一次性处理所有token计算上不可行。现有方法缺乏有效的稀疏选择机制来筛选与查询相关的视觉token。

Method: 设计一个可学习的嵌入（ReToken），将其训练为显式检索目标，从预填充的视觉KV缓存中稀疏选择与查询相关的视觉token。仅需在小型图像问答数据集上训练即可。

Result: 在Visual Haystacks上，Qwen3VL-8B提升13.4分，InternVL3.5提升12.4分（相对提升超过20%）；在LVBench上零样本迁移至长视频，Qwen3VL-8B获得8.0分增益。训练和长视频推理均在单个H100上完成。

Conclusion: ReToken通过轻量级可学习检索嵌入实现了高效稀疏视觉token选择，在不同模型和任务上展现出良好泛化性，兼具高性能与低计算成本优势。

Abstract: Long visual context poses a challenge for vision-language models: performance degrades as the number of distractors grows, and processing all tokens at once is computationally infeasible under GPU memory constraints. We present ReToken, a single learnable embedding trained as an explicit retrieval target that selects a sparse set of query-relevant visual tokens from a pre-filled visual KV cache. Trained on only a small image-QA dataset, ReToken yields consistent gains across image and video benchmarks: on Visual Haystacks it improves Qwen3VL-8B by 13.4 points and InternVL3.5 by 12.4 points (>20% relative), and on LVBench it transfers zero-shot to long video for an 8.0-point gain with Qwen3VL-8B. Thanks to its lightweight design, both training and long-video inference fit on a single H100. Code is available at: https://github.com/avaxiao/ReToken

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Recursive transformers for semiconductor thermo-mechanical reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27251)
*Kart-leong Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并系统评估了三种递归Transformer范式（Tiny Recursive模型、Depth Recursive模型和简单递归Transformer），用于替代工程设计中昂贵的一阶仿真。通过权重重共享减少可学习参数，在低维小数据集上避免了过拟合并降低了计算开销，在两个工程任务（半导体封装热机械可靠性分析和Laplace PDE电容场求解）中验证了其在预测精度、参数效率和计算成本之间提供有效的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer架构在小规模、低维工程数据集上过度参数化，导致过拟合和多余的内存/计算开销。大型仿真数据生成成本高昂，需要转向注重额外计算而非额外可学习参数的架构。

Method: 在热机械代理分析中系统比较三种递归Transformer架构：Tiny Recursive模型、提出的Depth Recursive Transformer和简单递归Transformer，通过权重重共享机制减少参数容量，并评估预测性能（Recall、MRR）、参数数量和FLOPs。

Result: 递归权重重共享Transformer在预测精度、参数效率和计算成本之间提供了有效且可泛化的权衡，适用于小数据工程代理建模场景。

Conclusion: 在资源受限场景下，递归Transformer架构为工程代理建模提供了实用的设计指南，验证了\"以计算换参数\"这一原则在低维工程预测任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Transformer-based surrogate models are increasingly used to replace expensive first-principles simulation in engineering design. But conventional transformer architectures are often over parameterized for the small, low-dimensional datasets typical of engineering design spaces, where large simulation data is expensive to generate. Under these conditions, excess parameter capacity leads to overfitting rather than improved accuracy, while also incurring unnecessary memory and compute overhead. This motivates a shift towards architectures that focus on additional compute rather than additional learnable parameters. This paper presents a hardware-aware evaluation of three recursive transformer paradigms for surrogate thermo-mechanical analysis of advanced packages: a)Tiny Recursive Model, b) our proposed Depth Recursive transformer, c) and a simple recursive transformer. We systematically compare their predictive performance (Recall, Mean Reciprocal Rank), parameter count, computational complexity (FLOPs), providing practical design guidelines for selecting recursive transformer architectures under resource-constrained scenarios. We validate this principle on two low-dimensional engineering prediction tasks: 1) thermo-mechanical reliability analysis of advanced semiconductor packages, where stress and warpage from thermal cycling must be evaluated repeatedly across a design-of-experiments sweep under costly finite element analysis (FEA). 2) Laplace PDE iterative numerical solver for capacitance field. Overall, recursive weight-sharing transformers provide an effective and generalizable trade-off between prediction accuracy, parameter efficiency, and computational cost for small data engineering surrogate modeling.

</details>


### [109] [Regularizing modality contribution drift in multimodal continual learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27260)
*Zhen Zhang,Jielei Chu,Bin Liu,Tianrui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出模态贡献漂移（MCD）问题，指多模态持续学习中各模态及其交互在增量任务上的相对贡献不稳定，并提出CMCDR正则化方法（含回放和无回放两种版本）来保持旧任务的模态贡献结构，在多种多模态持续学习任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MMCL方法主要关注跨模态表示对齐或语义相似性，但忽略了各模态的相对贡献及其交互在增量任务中是否保持稳定。这正是本文定义的模态贡献漂移（MCD）问题，现有方法无法可靠地缓解这一漂移。

Method: 1) 定义MCD分数，通过受控的模态子集干预量化贡献强度和相对依赖变化；2) 提出CMCDR正则化方法，保留先前任务的模态贡献结构；3) 回放版本利用冻结的旧模型对旧样本进行模态子集干预诊断，约束贡献变化；4) 无回放版本利用当前任务样本作为探针，蒸馏冻结模型的旧任务贡献响应。

Result: 在多模态类增量学习和持续视觉问答两类任务上进行实验，验证了CMCDR的通用性和有效性，同时理论分析解释了现有MMCL方法为何无法可靠缓解MCD。

Conclusion: CMCDR通过显式约束模态贡献结构的稳定性来缓解多模态持续学习中的遗忘问题，并针对是否有旧样本回放提供了两种适配版本，是首个从决策层面模态贡献稳定性角度解决MMCL的方法。

Abstract: Multimodal continual learning (MMCL) aims to learn emerging knowledge from multimodal data while preserving knowledge. To mitigate forgetting, current MMCL methods usually focus on cross-modal representation alignment or semantic similarity, but they overlook whether the relative contributions of individual modalities and their interactions remain stable across incremental tasks. We term this decision-level shift Modality Contribution Drift (MCD) and quantify it with the MCD score, which combines contribution-strength and relative-reliance changes under controlled interventions on modality subsets. Theoretical and empirical analyses further explain why current MMCL methods cannot reliably mitigate this drift. To this end, we propose Continual Modality Contribution Drift Regularization (CMCDR), which preserves the modality contribution structure of previously learned tasks. Since MMCL settings differ in whether old exemplars are available, CMCDR includes both replay-based and replay-free versions. The replay-based version uses modality-subset interventions as diagnostic probes on stored old samples, compares their contribution profiles between the current model and a frozen previous model, and constrains changes in old-sample modality-specific and interaction contributions. The replay-free version uses current-task samples as probes and distills the frozen model's old-task contribution responses, thereby regularizing the observed contribution profile without exemplars. Experiments on multimodal class-incremental learning and continual visual question answering validate the generality and effectiveness of CMCDR.

</details>


### [110] [DoTime: A Synthetic Benchmark Generator for Interventional and Counterfactual Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27263)
*Dennis Thumm,Billy Tim Anthony,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DoTime，一个开源、可扩展且理论上严谨的多变量时间序列结构因果模型（TSCM）生成器，支持干预和反事实采样，并附带四个冻结评测套件及基线实现，验证了干预训练相比纯观测模型在方向预测准确性上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列因果推断基准大多局限于观测数据、规模小或领域特定，缺乏对干预和反事实估计的充分支持，而这恰恰在医疗、政策评估和气候科学等关键领域最为重要。

Method: DoTime作为多变量时序SCM生成器，支持连续时间干预窗口、带阳性防护的反事实采样模式、作为中断时间序列严格泛化的机制切换SCM、天然非平稳动态、确定性斜坡和正弦干预轮廓。同时提供10万条轨迹的训练规模快照和八种命名识别结构，并实现因果基础模型的先验参考实现。

Result: 在结构匹配的保留时段评估中，干预先验拟合网络（PFN）与同容量观测模型相比，在每种结构、轨迹长度和训练种子下均呈现正的方向准确率差距。

Conclusion: 干预训练能够为时间序列因果模型带来可测量的方向预测准确率优势，DoTime可作为因果基础模型训练的有效数据先验，并提供一个可验证、可复现的评测平台。

Abstract: Most benchmarks for causal inference over time series are observational, small, or domain-specific, leaving interventional and counterfactual estimation under-served exactly where it matters most, such as in healthcare, policy evaluation, and climate science. We introduce \textbf{DoTime}, an open, scalable, and theoretically grounded generator of multivariate temporal structural causal models (TSCMs) with interventions, released as the \code{dotime} PyPI package together with four frozen evaluation suites. Beyond existing work, it adds capabilities absent from prior generators: continuous-time intervention \emph{windows}, counterfactual sampling modes with a positivity guard, regime-switching SCMs as a strict generalization of interrupted time series, non-stationary dynamics by construction with switching SCM parameters, and deterministic ramp and sinusoidal intervention profiles that place trends and structural breaks \emph{inside} the evaluation window. Moreover, it demonstrates the suitability of the generator as a prior for a causal foundation model reference implementation. The released suites span a training-scale snapshot of $100{,}000$ trajectories and eight named identification structures, each with exact ground truth: paired interventional trajectories from the same SCM throughout, and shared-noise counterfactuals in the continuous-time suite. We ship reference baseline implementations with an evaluation harness, and pose a falsifiable claim: interventional training buys a measurable direction-accuracy advantage over an observational model of identical capacity. It is tested across three training seeds per arm. Under structure-matched evaluation on held-out episodes, the interventional prior-fitted network's (PFN) gap is positive in every structure, trajectory length, and seed tested.

</details>


### [111] [PlatformBid: An Auto-Bidding Benchmark from a Unified Advertising Platform's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27265)
*Shengtian Yang,Yewen Li,Peng Jiang,Zhiyi Lyu,Bo An,Peng Jiang,Qingpeng Cai,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PlatformBid，这是首个从统一广告平台（整合SSP、DSP和Ad Exchange）视角出发的自动出价基准，定义了三种代表性场景（同质竞争、异质竞争、促销竞争），系统评估了多种现有方法，并提出基于流匹配的新方法BidFlow，在快手线上实验中目标成本提升0.68%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动出价算法仅从DSP（广告主）视角出发，最大化广告主转化率，但当前大型广告平台已内部整合SSP、DSP和Ad Exchange功能，平台视角下目标不仅是广告主转化，还有平台总收益。缺乏平台中心的评估框架阻碍了自动出价研究的发展。

Method: 提出PlatformBid基准框架，定义三种代表性设置：(1)同质竞争（所有广告主使用相同算法）；(2)异质竞争（广告主使用多样化算法策略）；(3)促销竞争（部分广告主在黑色星期五等促销活动期间增加预算）。系统评估经典控制方法、基于RL的方法和近期生成式方法，并提出基于流匹配（flow-matching）的新方法BidFlow。

Result: 在快手平台进行在线实验，BidFlow在目标成本上实现了+0.68%的改进，验证了PlatformBid离线-在线一致性。

Conclusion: PlatformBid作为首个平台视角的自动出价基准，为动态竞争环境下的自动出价研究提供了全面评估框架，BidFlow展现了流匹配方法在表达性策略表示上的优势，并证明了离线评估与在线部署的一致性。

Abstract: Real-time bidding is central to computational advertising, comprising three elements: Supply Side Platform (SSP) selling ad impressions, Demand Side Platform (DSP) bidding for advertisers, and Ad Exchange conducting auctions between them. Traditional auto-bidding algorithms focus solely on the DSP side, maximizing advertiser conversions by adjusting bids against competitors. However, current big ad platforms, such as social media and e-commerce companies, now integrate SSP, DSP, and Ad Exchange functions internally. From such ad platforms' perspective, the goal of the auto-bidding algorithms is not only to maximize the advertisers' conversions, but also the total revenue of the platform. Given the lack of platform-centric evaluation frameworks and the pressing need to advance auto-bidding research, we propose PlatformBid - the first comprehensive benchmark designed from a unified ad platform's perspective. To accurately reflect the real-world auto-bidding scenarios, we define three representative settings: (1) homogeneous competition with identical algorithms across advertisers, (2) heterogeneous competition with diverse algorithmic strategies, and (3) promotional competition where some advertisers surge budgets for boosting sales during promotional events like Black Friday. We systematically evaluate a broad spectrum of existing auto-bidding methods across these settings, encompassing classical control methods, RL-based methods, and recent generative methods. Besides these methods, we further propose a novel auto-bidding method based on flow-matching, termed BidFlow, which leverages the flow-matching method's expressive policy representation to effectively handle dynamic competitive environments. Online experiments on Kuaishou further show a +0.68\% improvement in target cost, providing deployment evidence for the offline-online consistency of PlatformBid.

</details>


### [112] [Beyond KV Reconstruction: Functional Reconstruction for MLA Draft Models in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27269)
*Weiye Shi,Fanxu Meng,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"功能重建"（Functional Reconstruction）的端到端后转换方法，用于在MHA/GQA注意力模型转换为MLA时，保留原有注意力模块的输出功能，从而避免转换后推测解码中草稿token接受率大幅下降的问题。该方法在96种配置下显著改善了37/64个任务单元的token接受率，且无需验证器logits或监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLA因紧凑的潜状态能减少长上下文推理中的KV缓存和内存流量而日益重要，但多数开源模型使用MHA/GQA，需要转换才能获得MLA的缓存效率。然而，直接转换会引入注意力函数误差（低秩分解和RoPE处理），虽然对独立生成影响不大，但会显著降低推测解码中草稿token的接受率，因此需要一种能保持原有注意力功能的转换方法。

Method: 提出端到端（E2E）功能重建方法：将每个转换后的MLA注意力模块优化为在校准隐状态上复现原始MHA/GQA对应模块在输出投影后的响应。该方法与具体转换器无关，作为后转换步骤执行，保留转换后的缓存和推理图，且不需要验证器logits或验证器监督。

Result: 在涵盖4个Llama/Qwen草稿-目标对、TransMLA和MHA2MLA两种转换器、HF和vLLM两个后端、4个200提示任务的192个模型-转换器-后端-方法-任务配置上评估。在0.5个百分点报告容差下，功能重建在64个匹配任务单元中显著改善了37个的接受率，26个基本不变，仅1个显著下降。

Conclusion: 将MHA/GQA到MLA的转换视为功能重建而非单纯的缓存压缩，可以显著提升转换后模型的推测解码性能，同时不牺牲MLA的缓存效率优势。该方法广泛适用于不同转换器和后端，且无需额外的验证器监督。

Abstract: Multi-head latent attention (MLA) is increasingly important for long-context LLM inference because compact latent states replace the growing key-value (KV) cache and reduce decoding memory traffic. Yet most capable open checkpoints use multi-head or grouped-query attention (MHA/GQA), so conversion is needed to obtain MLA's cache efficiency without retraining from scratch. Speculative decoding offers complementary acceleration, but its speedup depends on agreement between draft proposals and target verification. We find that direct MHA/GQA-to-MLA conversion can sharply reduce this agreement: low-rank factorization and RoPE handling introduce attention-function errors that may be tolerable for standalone generation but substantially lower draft-token acceptance. We therefore formulate MLA draft construction as functional reconstruction rather than cache compression. Our end-to-end (E2E) method optimizes each converted MLA attention module to reproduce the post-output-projection response of its original MHA/GQA counterpart on calibration hidden states. This converter-agnostic post-conversion procedure preserves the converted cache and inference graph and requires neither verifier logits nor verifier supervision. We evaluate 192 model-converter-backend-method-task configurations spanning four Llama/Qwen draft-target pairs, TransMLA and MHA2MLA, HF and vLLM, and four 200-prompt tasks. With a 0.5-percentage-point reporting tolerance, Functional Reconstruction materially improves acceptance in 37 of 64 matched task cells, leaves 26 practically unchanged, and materially decreases one. Code and evaluation artifacts are available at https://github.com/swyhahaha/FunctionalMLA.

</details>


### [113] [RLPF: Reinforcement Learning from Performance Feedback for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27271)
*Huihao Jing,Haozhe Cui,Wenbin Hu,Shaojin Chen,Haochen Shi,Changxuan Fan,Yuxuan Liu,Hanyu Yang,Sirui Zhang,Ziyi Chen,Haoran Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RLPF（基于性能反馈的强化学习），通过将执行结果转化为分阶段奖励来训练代码智能体生成更快的正确程序。在PerfCodeBench上，将Qwen3-32B的正确且可运行率从11.1%提升到54.6%，相对效率从8.1%提升到38.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码模型的训练信号大多停留在正确性层面，忽略了运行效率。两个程序可能通过相同的测试但在运行时间上差异巨大，而运行时间本身是脆弱的奖励信号，只有在程序正确后才有意义，且在不同任务间差异大，当多数采样程序无法编译或运行时难以提供有效指导。

Method: 提出RLPF（Reinforcement Learning from Performance Feedback），将执行结果转化为分阶段奖励：失败的程序按执行进度排序，正确的程序则根据其相对基线到专家参考的改进程度进行排名。这使得在正确性之前有有用的反馈，在正确性之后有性能敏感的反馈。

Result: 对Qwen3-32B进行RLPF微调后，在PerfCodeBench上正确且可运行的解决方案从11.1%提升至54.6%，相对效率从8.1%提升至38.6%。训练后的模型与更强的开放权重系统竞争力相当，优化行为可适度迁移到EffiBench-X。额外研究表明模型生成的参考提供有用但较弱的监督，完整的复合奖励比仅正确性或仅运行时间的基线更可靠。

Conclusion: 代码智能体不仅可以被训练来通过测试，还可以被训练来优化其编写的程序，这表明性能反馈可以作为有效的训练信号，而不仅仅是评估指标。

Abstract: Code models are increasingly trained with execution feedback, but most training signals still stop at correctness. This leaves an important gap for systems code: two programs can pass the same tests while differing greatly in runtime. We study how to train code agents to prefer faster correct implementations, rather than treating efficiency only as an evaluation metric. The key difficulty is that runtime is a fragile reward. It is meaningful only after a program is correct, varies across tasks, and gives little guidance when most sampled programs fail to compile or run. We propose \textbf{RLPF}, reinforcement learning from performance feedback, which turns execution outcomes into a staged reward. Failed programs are ordered by execution progress, while correct programs are ranked by their relative improvement from the baseline toward the expert reference. This gives useful feedback before correctness and performance-sensitive feedback after correctness. Fine-tuning Qwen3-32B with RLPF on PerfCodeBench raises correct-and-runnable solutions from $11.1\%$ to $54.6\%$ and improves relative efficiency from $8.1\%$ to $38.6\%$. The trained model becomes competitive with stronger open-weight systems, and its optimization behavior transfers modestly to EffiBench-X. Additional studies show that model-generated references provide useful but weaker supervision, and that the full composite reward is more reliable than correctness-only or runtime-only baselines. These results suggest that code agents can be trained not only to pass tests, but also to optimize the programs they write.

</details>


### [114] [SDO: Structure-Aware Data Organization for Efficient LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27273)
*Jinliang Gao,Ning Yang,Hai Wang,Baili Xiao,Pin Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SDO框架，通过曝光驱动的数据组织机制（基于外部嵌入的KNN邻域批次形成和跨epoch的曝光均衡调度）来优化大语言模型后训练的样本组织和曝光，加速收敛并提升各问题类型的准确率均衡性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型后训练的效率优化主要聚焦于样本选择和训练调度，但数据组织本身被视为静态预处理步骤，无法适应优化过程中样本曝光的动态变化。固定分区的分组方法导致所有样本获得相似的曝光度，忽略了不同样本的优化需求差异，造成部分样本冗余更新而其他样本优化不足。

Method: 提出SDO（Structure-Aware Data Organization），一个即插即用的数据组织框架，包含曝光驱动的反馈机制。具体包括：（1）epoch内，基于KNN邻域遍历的局部感知批次形成，构建连贯的mini-batch；（2）epoch间，曝光均衡调度记录每个样本的参与次数，降低过度曝光样本的采样概率以保持长期覆盖。SDO在冻结的外部嵌入上逐epoch运行，避免模型预热训练开销。

Result: 在SFT、DPO和GRPO三种训练范式中，SDO均加速了收敛，且在训练早期到中期阶段提升最为显著。该方法产生了更连贯的梯度，并在不同问题类型间实现了更均衡的准确率，同时不会永久排除任何训练样本。

Conclusion: SDO通过将数据组织从静态预处理转变为动态、曝光感知的过程，有效解决了样本曝光不均衡的问题，在不牺牲样本覆盖的前提下提升了后训练效率和效果，是一个通用且即插即用的数据组织方案。

Abstract: Post-training of large language models is expensive, and existing efficiency improvements mainly focus on selecting informative samples or designing training schedules. However, data organization itself is usually treated as a static preprocessing step: embedding-based grouping methods construct fixed partitions before training and cannot adapt to the evolving sample exposure during optimization. As a result, all samples receive similar exposure despite their different optimization needs, leading to redundant updates for some samples while leaving others under-optimized. To address this problem, we propose SDO (Structure-Aware Data Organization), a plug-and-play data organization framework with an exposure-driven feedback mechanism that organizes mini-batch composition and sample exposure according to representation-space structure. SDO operates epoch by epoch on frozen external embeddings, avoiding model warm-up training overhead: within each epoch, locality-aware batching forms coherent mini-batches via KNN neighborhood traversal; across epochs, exposure-balanced scheduling records per-sample participation and reduces the sampling probability of over-exposed samples to preserve long-term coverage. Across SFT, DPO, and GRPO, SDO accelerates convergence, with the largest gains observed in the early-to-mid phase, producing more coherent gradients and more balanced accuracy across question types without permanently excluding training samples.

</details>


### [115] [Rethinking EEG-Based Disease Diagnosis: Decoupling Instance Representation Learning from Subject-Level Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27274)
*Zhiyuan Ma,Zeyuan Li,Zhiyi Lu,Jiacheng Hao,Youlang Du,Zhen Jiang,Xinche Zhang,Yuhao Sun,Sen Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BridgeMIL，一个两阶段框架，将EEG实例表示学习与受试者级监督解耦，通过无继承标签的预训练和注意力MIL聚合器完成EEG疾病诊断，在三个数据集、五个骨干网络上平均准确率达76.57%，比最强基线高4.28个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG疾病诊断需要每个受试者一个预测，但现有管线将记录分割成实例并继承受试者标签进行实例级分类，假设所有实例提供同等可靠的诊断证据，这一假设不合理。MIL虽能避免继承标签，但EEG数据集中受试者远少于实例，限制了端到端MIL学习的表征质量。

Method: 提出BridgeMIL两阶段框架：阶段1在不使用继承实例标签的情况下预训练编码器，通过对齐时间邻近窗口和独立采样的受试者内子袋来学习表征，利用方差和协方差正则化防止坍塌并减少冗余（无需负样本对）；阶段2将编码器迁移到基于注意力的MIL聚合器，仅对受试者预测施加监督，并通过特征保持机制限制表征漂移。

Result: 在三个EEG疾病数据集和五个代表性骨干网络上，BridgeMIL在15个数据集-骨干组合中的14个取得最高平均准确率，总体平均准确率76.57%，比最强基线高4.28个百分点。分析还发现实例继承标签可靠性存在显著差异，性能对受试者稀缺更敏感（而非实例稀缺），且表征空间更结构化，具有清晰的受试者聚类和改善的诊断类别分离。

Conclusion: 将监督与受试者级别预测目标对齐的同时从丰富的EEG实例中学习（不向单个实例分配疾病标签）是更有效的EEG疾病诊断范式，BridgeMIL验证了这一思路的有效性。

Abstract: EEG-based disease diagnosis requires one prediction per subject, yet common pipelines segment recordings into short instances, inherit the subject label for every instance, and train instance-level classifiers. This assumes that all instances provide equally reliable diagnostic evidence. Multiple instance learning (MIL) avoids inherited labels by treating each subject as a bag. However, EEG datasets contain far fewer subjects than instances, which can limit the quality of the representations learned by end-to-end MIL. We propose BridgeMIL, a two-stage framework that decouples instance representation learning from subject-level supervision. Stage 1 pretrains the encoder without inherited instance labels by aligning temporally nearby windows and independently sampled within-subject sub-bags. Variance and covariance regularization prevent collapse and reduce redundancy without negative pairs. Stage 2 transfers the encoder to an attention-based MIL aggregator, applies supervision only to subject predictions, and limits representation drift through feature retention. Across three EEG disease datasets and five representative backbones, BridgeMIL attains the highest mean accuracy in 14 of 15 dataset-backbone settings and an overall mean accuracy of 76.57%, 4.28 percentage points higher than the strongest baseline. Further analyses reveal substantial variation in inherited-label reliability across instances, greater performance sensitivity to subject scarcity than to instance scarcity, and a more structured representation space with distinct subject-wise clusters and improved separation between diagnostic classes. Together, these findings underscore the importance of aligning supervision with the subject-level prediction objective while learning from abundant EEG instances without assigning disease labels to individual instances.

</details>


### [116] [Flat Score, Amplified Failures: How the Error Budget Masks Damage in Quantized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27275)
*Jiwon Jang,Kisu Yang,Heuiseok Lim,Hyunwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究4-bit权重量化对多轮工具调用智能体的影响，发现虽然标准指标上量化看似无损，但量化会放大模型已有的失败模式（如工具名称幻觉），最多放大2.5倍。标准分数不变是因为基准测试的十错误预算掩盖了额外失败。作者提出两种诊断方法（每通道错误率和缩小预算下的成功率）来暴露量化损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4-bit权重量化被广泛报告为几乎无损，但现有评估可能未覆盖多轮工具调用智能体这一关键场景。作者想验证这一说法在最需要量化性能的agent任务中是否成立，并揭示标准指标可能掩盖的真实损坏。

Method: 在τ²-bench基准上，对两个开源模型家族（dense和MoE变体）在两个领域共8个单元（每单元456个episode），在16-bit、8-bit和4-bit三种权重精度下进行系统评估。使用等价性检验、多重比较校正、秩相关分析，并引入两种诊断方法：每通道错误率和缩小错误预算下的成功率。还进行了针对性错误修复提示实验。

Result: 标准指标上没有任何单元显示出能通过多重比较校正的分数变化，最严重的单元中变化在±7.5分内。但量化将已有失败（电信领域工具名称幻觉、零售领域实体错误）体积放大至2.5倍（每任务+17.6分），几乎不产生新失败（失败集秩相关≥0.94，仅0.18%新事件）。将错误预算从10缩小到2时重新暴露17分的分数差距，且仅出现在量化增加错误量的单元中。

Conclusion: 量化不是无损的——它在标准指标上看起来无损只是因为基准的错误预算掩盖了实际的失败放大。作者建议在报告任务奖励的同时，报告每通道错误率和缩小错误预算下的成功率作为诊断指标。

Abstract: Post-training quantization to 4-bit weights is widely reported to be nearly lossless. We test this claim for multi-turn, tool-calling agents, where it now matters most. On $τ^2$-bench, across two open-weight model families in dense and MoE variants and two domains (eight cells, 456 episodes each, at 16-, 8-, and 4-bit weights), quantization indeed looks free on the standard metric. No cell shows a score change that survives multiple-comparison correction, and in the cell that carries the largest process damage, equivalence testing bounds the change within $\pm$7.5 points. The process tells a different story. Quantization amplifies the failure the model already exhibits at full precision (tool-name hallucination in telecom, with the same directional trend in retail entity errors) by up to 2.5$\times$ in volume (+17.6 points per task), while creating essentially no new failures. The failure set is the same at every precision (rank correlation $\geq$ 0.94, 0.18% novel events). The score stays flat because the benchmark's ten-error budget absorbs the extra failures. Shrinking the budget to two errors re-exposes a score gap of 17 points, and it does so only in the one cell where quantization added error volume, exactly as the masking account predicts. A targeted error-repair prompt, run for five telecom models at every precision, removes the damage exactly and only where it lives. Both diagnostics, the per-channel error rate and success under a shrinking budget, come from logs benchmarks already collect; we suggest reporting them alongside task reward.

</details>


### [117] [The Kinetics of Training: A Driven-Nucleation Rate Law for Emergence, Plasticity Loss, and Circuit Control in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27281)
*Lei Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现语言模型能力的形成受限于"无部分信用"的联合对齐机制——电路的多个部分必须在一个随机尝试中同时正确对齐，差一个都不行。通过多种实验（Pythia模型、门控注意力模型等）验证了这一机制，并提出通过重新初始化query-key切片来恢复可学习性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型能力涌现的底层机制，特别是为什么能力会突然出现而非渐进提升。作者发现现有监控方法（如验证损失）对能力形成过程"失明"，需要更精确的理论框架来解释和预测能力涌现。

Method: 通过三组预注册实验从三个方向验证联合对齐是能力形成的限速步骤：1）正向实验：在混合浓度达到阈值后能力突然点燃，并可从前体预测到达时间；2）反向实验：延迟学习会随着等待时间增长而变得不可能，尽管验证损失平滑下降；3）机制验证：在门控注意力模型中证明占据效应强制产生截止期限。此外还研究了破坏位置（头部承诺于基数据）及修复方法（重新初始化query-key切片）。

Result: 五部分电路缺少三部分的等待时间与三部分电路缺少三部分相当（比率1.19-1.37），说明等待时间取决于缺失部分数量而非电路规模；在32/32个判别单元中，消融一个部分后仅保留中位数17%的能力（部分信用预测为50-83%，p=2e-10）；重新初始化query-key切片可恢复6/6的可学习性，而value切片无效（0/6）。

Conclusion: 能力形成需要一个稀有事件（多部分联合对齐），其势垒随缺失部分数量增长。这产生了统一速率方程（位点×尝试×驱动×exp(-βK)减去破坏），可从三个方向读取。SGD的噪声无法通过涨落-耗散检验，因此需要安装适当的噪声源并进行退火来按计划熔化、固定电路。

Abstract: A capability appears in a language model when the last parts of its circuit align in one stochastic attempt, and getting all but one right is worth nothing. We show this no-partial-credit joint alignment is the rate-limiting step of capability formation. Two fingerprints: in a shortcut-free apparatus a five-part circuit missing three waits as long as a three-part circuit missing three (1.19-1.37), so the wait counts missing parts, not size; and on Pythia across seven capabilities and three scales, ablating one part leaves a median 17% of the capability in 32 of 32 discriminating cells, where partial credit predicts 50-83% (p = 2e-10), while a random non-part head leaves 100%.
  One rare event whose barrier grows with missing parts yields a rate equation -- sites x attempts x drive x exp(-beta*K), minus destruction -- read three ways, each preregistered with frozen constants. Forward: a capability flat at baseline ignites at a step of our choosing once the mix passes a concentration floor (10/10 above, 0/12 below), and while still flat its arrival is datable from its precursor to 5% median error on six held-out models. Backward: the delay to learn a withheld capability grows with waiting until, past a critical step, it never ignites -- yet validation loss falls smoothly throughout, so standard monitors are blind to it. We locate the damage (heads commit to the base data) and isolate the cure: re-initializing only the query-key slices restores learnability (6/6) while the value slices do nothing (0/6). We prove the mechanism in a controlled gated-attention model: occupation forces a deadline whose consequences need no mixing assumption. Completed: SGD's noise fails the fluctuation-dissipation test, so we install one and anneal, melt and pin circuits on schedule. Scope: conjunction circuits in transformers to 1.4B.

</details>


### [118] [Benchmarking the Residual: What Long-Horizon Evaluations Add Beyond Matched Short-Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27283)
*Chao Peng,Zhiheng Lyu,Peijie Dong,Hande Dong,Qiang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"horizon residual"（水平残差）指标，用于区分长时程任务失败是由阶段错误累积还是由"轨迹诱导退化"（先前执行使后续工作更难）导致，并主张基准测试必须将完整任务成功率与基于短阶段构建的基线预测进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程基准测试普遍显示任务越长智能体失败越多，但现有方法无法解释失败的根本原因：是普通错误随阶段数累积，还是早期执行使后续工作更困难（即轨迹诱导退化/上下文腐烂）。缺乏区分这些因素的严格方法。

Method: 提出必须将完整任务的实际成功率与从短、独立阶段构建的基线预测进行对比，计算二者对数比值作为"horizon residual"。该比较需使用相同智能体配置，并预先明确阶段划分、检查点、信息和预算的选取方式。

Result: horizon residual能够揭示完整任务表现与基线预测之间的偏差，表明完整轨迹不同于所选基线；但要解释偏差的具体成因，仍需针对性的消融实验。

Conclusion: 要声称存在"长时程失败"，必须通过上述基线对比来验证；residual只诊断是否存在偏离，不直接解释原因，需要进一步实验定位。

Abstract: Long-horizon benchmarks often show that agents fail more as tasks become longer. This observation is useful for deployment, but it does not by itself explain why failure occurs. More stages create more opportunities for ordinary errors to compound; longer tasks may also contain harder individual decisions or become harder as conversation history, tool outputs, and environment changes accumulate. We use trajectory-induced degradation to mean this last possibility: earlier execution makes later work harder. When the harmful accumulation is specifically the text visible to the model, it is often called context rot. In this position paper, we argue that to claim a "long-horizon failure", benchmarks must compare actual full-task success against a baseline prediction built from short, individual stages. We call the log-ratio between this prediction and actual success the horizon residual. The comparison must use the same agent configuration and specify in advance how stages, checkpoints, information, and budgets will be chosen. The residual shows that the full rollout differs from the chosen baseline; targeted experiments are still needed to explain why.

</details>


### [119] [TIER-MoE: Trust-Informed Expert Routing via Conditional Modality Risk for Multimodal Fusion in Biomedical Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27289)
*Yu Chang,Anzhe Cheng,Chenwei Wu,Zhuoran Wang,Jiahao Chen,Tamoghna Chattopadhyay,Sophia I. Thomopoulos,Paul M. Thompson,Liyue Shen,Paul Bogdan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TIER-MoE是一个风险引导的子空间专家混合模型，通过估计每个样本的模态可靠性（即单模态预测器的预期损失），结合专家子空间兼容性进行稀疏模态-专家路由，同时保留共享路径以维持多模态互补性，在四个公共多模态生物医学数据集上取得了优于现有方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态融合的核心在于整合互补的证据来源，但更多证据并不总能带来更好的预测。现有方法虽然通过跨模态交互和样本自适应融合取得了进展，但融合过程中分配给某模态的权重并不能揭示该来源是否不可靠、冗余或与专业专家不匹配。为此需要一种能够显式建模样本级模态可靠性的机制。

Method: TIER-MoE将样本特定的模态可靠性定义为该模态单模态预测器预期产生的预测损失，该风险通过未在对应样本上训练过的模型产生的折外预测来学习。模型将估计的风险与专家特定的子空间兼容性相结合，进行稀疏模态-专家路由；同时，始终激活的共享路径保留多模态互补性。

Result: 在四个公共多模态生物医学数据集（涵盖阿尔茨海默病状态、皮肤病变恶性程度和视网膜分类）上的实验表明，TIER-MoE在预测性能和概率校准方面均优于现有最先进方法，在Macro-F1和Brier分数上保持一致改进，并对外部队列展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过风险引导的模态可靠性估计与子空间专家路由机制，TIER-MoE有效解决了多模态融合中"更多证据不一定带来更好预测"的问题，在生物医学多模态任务上显著提升了预测性能与校准质量。

Abstract: The promise of multimodal fusion lies in combining complementary sources of evidence, yet more evidence does not always yield a better prediction. Recent multimodal models have advanced fusion through richer cross-modal interaction and sample-adaptive fusion. However, the influence assigned to a modality during fusion does not reveal whether that source is unreliable, redundant, or poorly matched to a specialized expert. To address this limitation, we introduce TIER-MoE, a risk-guided subspace mixture-of-experts model that defines sample-specific modality reliability as the prediction loss its unimodal predictor is expected to incur. This risk is learned from out-of-fold predictions generated by models that were not trained on the corresponding sample. TIER-MoE combines the estimated risk with expert-specific subspace compatibility for sparse modality-expert routing, while an always-active shared path preserves multimodal complementarity. We evaluate TIER-MoE on four public multimodal biomedical datasets spanning Alzheimer's disease status, skin-lesion malignancy, and retinal classification. Results demonstrate its superiority over state-of-the-art methods in predictive performance and probability calibration, with consistent improvements in Macro-F1 and Brier score and strong zero-shot generalization to an external cohort.

</details>


### [120] [EvoCause: LLM-Guided Evolution of Causal Graphs for Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27290)
*Lei Zan,Keli Zhang,Shifeng Xie,Jiale Zheng,Zehao Xiao,Zhiwei Dong,Ke Zhang,Ruichu Cai,Malik Tiomoko,Lujia Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoCause，一种利用大语言模型（LLM）和专家标注来迭代优化因果图的根因分析方法。EvoCause通过LLM提出语义合理的图编辑建议，并用确定性代码验证节点身份和acyclicity，在标注集上保留最优图。同时发布TeleRCA基准数据集，相关实验验证了EvoCause在合成数据和真实数据上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有根因分析方法从观测日志学习因果图后固定不变，无法利用历史事件中专家诊断的知识来改进图结构，导致诊断精度受限。

Method: EvoCause包含两个核心组件：(1) LLM根据专家标签提出语义合理的图编辑建议；(2) 确定性代码验证编辑的节点身份和acyclicity，并在标注对齐集上保留性能最优的图。测试时仅使用优化后的图进行透明预测，无需调用LLM。此外，作者发布了TeleRCA基准数据集，包含485,681条告警事件、194种告警类型和5,621个资源。

Result: 在合成数据上，以PC算法初始化的EvoCause相比未优化的PC基线，Node F1提升11.59个百分点，Case EM提升9.40个百分点，Graph F1提升4.59个百分点，nSHD降低0.2379。在TeleRCA上，将可读告警标题替换为匿名标识符后，Node F1和Case EM分别下降6.12和8.04个百分点，表明告警名称信息对图优化有重要贡献。

Conclusion: EvoCause成功地将专家知识融入因果图优化闭环，通过LLM与确定性验证相结合的方式实现了图结构的有效改进，且推理阶段无需LLM调用，兼顾了效果与效率。告警名称的语义信息对于图优化至关重要。

Abstract: Modern telecommunication, cloud, and microservice systems emit correlated alarm cascades when components fail. Root cause analysis (RCA) aims to identify the small set of alarms that initiate each cascade. A common approach learns a causal graph from observational logs and predicts all zero-in-degree alarms in each incident-induced subgraph. However, the learned graph remains fixed and cannot benefit from expert diagnoses of historical incidents. We close this loop with EvoCause. Expert labels constrain which alarms should be source nodes but do not specify the edge edits needed to satisfy those constraints. EvoCause uses a large language model (LLM) to propose semantically plausible graph edits, while deterministic code validates node identities and acyclicity and retains the best graph on a labeled alignment set. At test time, the refined graph alone produces transparent predictions without an LLM call. We also release TeleRCA, an expert-annotated benchmark from a production telecommunication network containing $485{,}681$ alarm events spanning $194$ alarm types over $5{,}621$ resources. On synthetic data, EvoCause initialized with the PC causal discovery algorithm outperforms the unrefined PC baseline, raising Node F1, Case EM, and Graph F1 by $11.59$, $9.40$, and $4.59$ percentage points, respectively, while reducing nSHD by $0.2379$. On TeleRCA, replacing human-readable alarm titles with anonymous identifiers lowers Node F1 and Case EM by $6.12$ and $8.04$ percentage points, respectively, indicating that alarm-name information contributes to graph refinement.

</details>


### [121] [THGFM: Dual-Branch Temporal Heterogeneous Graph Fusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27303)
*Yixin Peng,Diego Collarana,Er Jin,Stefan Decker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出THGFM，一个面向Web规模时序异构图的双路径融合模型，通过共享-专用双分支架构和旋转时序注意力机制，在学术图基准上平均提升+3.25%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时兼顾跨类型参数高效迁移和关系感知特化，且时间信息仅作为加性特征注入注意力核外部，缺乏对时间动态与图结构异质性的联合建模能力。

Method: THGFM采用统一双路径架构：(1) 共享空间时序注意力分支实现跨类型参数高效迁移；(2) 关系类型分区时序注意力分支实现关系感知特化；(3) 通过类型条件非竞争门控求和融合机制整合两分支；(4) 引入旋转时序注意力，将相对时间以半相位旋转方式直接嵌入注意力评分计算。

Result: 在多个学术图基准上一致优于基线图Transformer模型：六任务平均提升+3.25%，OAG-CS PV上峰值提升+12.37%，PF-L2提升+4.87%，PF-L1提升+1.18%，OGBN-MAG提升+4.24%，HTAG-ArXiv提升+3.73%，HTAG-DBLP提升+4.61%。

Conclusion: THGFM通过参数高效共享与关系特化并行的双分支设计，以及将时间直接融入注意力核的旋转机制，有效解决了时序异构图学习中的核心挑战，验证了融合架构在Web规模图数据上的优越性。

Abstract: Temporal heterogeneous graphs offer a natural abstraction for dynamic relational systems in which diverse node and relation types co-exist and evolve over time. Learning on such graphs requires jointly modeling cross-type structural heterogeneity and the temporal dynamics of interactions, yet existing methods still struggle to reconcile parameter-efficient cross-type transfer with relation-aware specialization, and typically inject time only as additive features outside the attention kernel. We propose \textbf{THGFM}, a web-scale temporal heterogeneous graph fusion model that addresses both limitations within a unified dual-path architecture. THGFM couples a \textit{Shared-Space Temporal Attention} branch for parameter-efficient cross-type transfer with a \textit{Relational Type-Partitioned Temporal Attention} branch for relation-aware specialization, and integrates them through \textit{Dual-Path Relational--Shared Fusion}, instantiated with \textit{Type-Conditioned Non-Competitive Gated Sum Fusion}: a adaptive mechanism that assigns independent, type-conditioned feature-wise gates to the shared and specialized branches, allowing both to be amplified or suppressed without zero-sum competition. To directly incorporate relative time into the attention score, THGFM further introduces \textit{Rotary Temporal Attention}, which rotates queries and keys by half-phases of relative time before matching. THGFM consistently outperforms baseline graph transformer models on academic graphs benchmarks, delivering a $+3.25\%$ six-task mean gain, with peak relative gains of $+12.37\%$ on OAG-CS PV, $+4.87\%$ on PF-$L_2$, and $+1.18\%$ on PF-$L_1$, and $+4.24\%$, $+3.73\%$, and $+4.61\%$ on OGBN-MAG, HTAG-ArXiv, and HTAG-DBLP, respectively.

</details>


### [122] [Position, Not Provenance: Separating Reasoning Mediation from Sycophancy in Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27304)
*Supratik Bhowal,Subhrajyoti Basu,Aritra Gir Mahanta,Anik Pal Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoT-Mediate行为框架，通过扰动模型自身生成的推理链来检验医学视觉语言模型的思维链推理是否真正因果影响预测结果。研究发现前缀强制续写比重新提示更能忠实反映推理介入，上下文位置而非声明来源是决定模型是否使用其生成推理的主要因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型在回答临床问题前会生成思维链推理，但现有研究未明确这种推理是否因果地影响模型预测。若推理只是"装饰性"输出而非真正驱动预测，将影响模型的可解释性和临床可靠性。作者旨在建立一个行为框架来严格检验这一因果关系。

Method: 提出CoT-Mediate框架，包含两部分：(1)双分支协议，对比重新提示证据与前缀强制续写两种推理注入方式；(2)来源控制干预，在保持推理内容完全一致的情况下仅变化其属性来源，以区分推理中介效应与谄媚效应。在LLaVA-Med和MedGemma两个模型上各评估1,000个VQA-RAD样本。

Result: 前缀强制续写始终比重新提示产生更高的中介忠实度；来源分析揭示两个模型具有不同的服从行为模式；在两个模型中，移除视觉证据会增加对注入推理的依赖；侧别（laterality）是最不忠实的临床属性。

Conclusion: 推理注入的机制显著影响测量的忠实度，上下文位置而非声明的来源是医学VLM是否使用其生成推理的主要决定因素。这提示评估医学VLM推理可靠性的方式需要更加关注推理出现的位置和方式。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) generate chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning before answering clinical questions, but whether this reasoning causally influences predictions remains unclear. We present CoT-Mediate, a behavioral framework that perturbs a single clinically meaningful attribute within a model's own generated reasoning and measures whether the resulting prediction follows the edited reasoning. Our framework combines a dual-arm protocol comparing re-prompted evidence with prefix-forced continuation, together with a provenance-controlled intervention that varies only the attributed source of identical reasoning to disentangle reasoning mediation from sycophancy. We evaluate LLaVA-Med and MedGemma on 1,000 VQA-RAD samples each. Prefix-forced continuation consistently yields higher mediation faithfulness than re-prompting, while the provenance analysis reveals distinct model-specific deference behaviors. Across both models, removing visual evidence increases reliance on injected reasoning, whereas laterality is the least faithfully tracked clinical attribute. These results show that the mechanism used to inject reasoning substantially affects measured faithfulness and that contextual position, rather than stated provenance, is the primary determinant of whether medical VLMs use their generated reasoning.

</details>


### [123] [ZUNA1.1: A more flexible EEG foundation model for Denoising and Super-resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27308)
*Christopher Warner,Jonas Mago,JR Huml,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ZUNA1.1是一个380M参数的扩散自编码器，用于灵活的大脑电信号（EEG）重建，支持可变长度（最长30秒）、任意通道数和任意头皮位置的信号重建，性能与前代模型相当且远超传统插值方法，并已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG重建方法（如球面样条插值）功能有限，无法灵活处理不同长度、不同通道数量和任意头皮位置的信号重建任务，需要一种更灵活的解决方案。

Method: 采用380M参数的扩散自编码器架构，能够重构最长30秒的可变长度序列、任意数量及位置的EEG通道，并支持通道内任意时间区间的重构。

Result: 性能至少与之前版本ZUNA1相当，同时具备更强的灵活性；在多种重建任务上持续显著优于球面样条插值等标准EEG去噪与重建方法。

Conclusion: ZUNA1.1在保持高重建性能的同时大幅提升了灵活性和适用场景，并以Apache 2.0许可开源发布，为EEG信号重建领域提供了更强大的工具。

Abstract: We introduce ZUNA1.1, a 380M-parameter diffusion autoencoder for flexible EEG signal reconstruction. ZUNA1.1 is capable of reconstructing variable length sequences of up to 30s, with an arbitrary number of EEG channels at arbitrary scalp locations, and can reconstruct arbitrary temporal intervals within channels in addition to reconstructing entire channels. We demonstrate that ZUNA1.1 performs at least on par with our earlier ZUNA1 model, while being far more flexible and capable of handling a wide range of reconstruction tasks. ZUNA1.1 continues to substantially outperform standard EEG denoising and reconstruction methods such as spherical spline interpolation, which is ubiquitously deployed in the MNE package. The ZUNA1.1 model is released open source under the permissive Apache 2.0 license.

</details>


### [124] [Modeling Decisions in Blockchain Analytics: A Leakage-Aware Evaluation of Tree-Based vs. Sequential Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27350)
*Michał Bartnicki,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种泄漏感知的以太坊Sybil机器人检测框架，包含Blind-Spot协议和Transaction Grammar表示方法，发现XGBoost在减少标签泄漏后优于Transformer序列模型，且延迟和能耗更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在Sybil机器人检测中虽表现好，但存在两个问题：计算代价高不适合实时监控，且由于高信号智能合约导致的标签泄漏使性能虚高。因此需要探索更可靠且实用的检测方法。

Method: 提出Blind-Spot协议消除与高信号合约相关的捷径，并设计Transaction Grammar表示钱包行为的节奏、EVM执行结构和意图；对比Transformer和BiLSTM序列模型与XGBoost和SVM基线。

Result: 在泄漏感知评估下，XGBoost在以太坊行为体分类任务上优于基于Transformer的序列模型，同时具有更低的延迟和估计能耗。

Conclusion: 泄漏感知评估揭示了序列模型性能可能被高估，树模型在实用性和效果上更具优势；该框架为后续以太坊行为体分类提供了基础。

Abstract: Sybil bots are Ethereum actors that imitate legitimate users to extract airdrop rewards or influence governance. Recent Sybil detection methods increasingly use deep learning and treat blockchain activity as a quasi-linguistic sequence. However, complex sequence models are computationally expensive for real-time monitoring, and their reported performance may be inflated by label leakage from high-signal smart contracts. We ask whether and how organic users, Sybil bots, and MEV bots differ in the structural complexity of their transaction histories; whether sequential models outperform tree-based tabular models once leakage is reduced; whether transaction order or timing provides the stronger behavioral signal; and whether the resulting models are practical for low-latency deployment. Our approach to leakage-aware Sybil bot detection consists of a Blind-Spot protocol and a Transaction Grammar representation of wallet behavior. The former eliminates shortcuts associated with high-signal contracts, whereas the latter models wallets using rhythm, EVM execution structure, and intent. We evaluate this approach on Ethereum actor classification by comparing Transformer and BiLSTM sequence models against XGBoost and SVM baselines. We contribute a framework for leakage-aware Ethereum actor classification and a Transaction Grammar representation of wallet behavior. Our results demonstrate that, under leakage-aware evaluation, XGBoost outperforms Transformer-based sequence models while providing lower latency and estimated energy use.

</details>


### [125] [Compression-Based Behavioral Similarity for Open-World Sybil Discovery on Ethereum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27370)
*Michał Bartnicki,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于压缩相似度的Sybil攻击者检测框架，通过从EVM交易轨迹中合成符号化"交易语法"，利用Gzip NCD构建行为图谱，无需监督训练即可在开放世界区块链审计中发现Sybil候选者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Sybil检测方法存在两大局限：基于图的方案依赖钱包间的代币转账（直接金融联系），而机器学习方法将其视为闭集分类问题，难以应对攻击策略的频繁变化和逃避战术。作者希望解决无需直接金融联系即可区分Sybil机器人、有机用户和套利机器人钱包的问题，并探究行为图在时间漂移和对抗扰动下的鲁棒性。

Method: 从EVM交易轨迹中合成符号化交易语法，分别捕获交易节奏、执行结构和功能意图三个维度；通过自研的盲点协议（Blind-Spot Protocol）过滤高信号合约；使用基于Gzip的NCD（归一化压缩距离）构建行为图谱用于Sybil发现。

Result: 该框架在监督式机器学习基线、时间分割测试和合成伪装压力测试中得到验证，核心NCD原语无需监督训练即可扩展可疑种子钱包，且不依赖显式的资金关联。

Conclusion: 作者贡献了一个泄漏感知（leakage-aware）的Sybil候选发现行为框架，将其定位为无需训练的本地发现原语，适用于开放世界区块链审计场景，而非正式的开放集识别系统。

Abstract: Sybil attackers are Blockchain actors that adopt the characteristics of regular users to exploit airdrops or influence governance. Current methods of Sybil actor detection include constructing graphs, which requires token transfers between examined wallets. Machine learning algorithms have been employed as well, but they treat the task as a closed-set classification problem, making them vulnerable to frequent changes in attack strategies or evasion tactics. We address the following questions: can compression-based similarity differentiate Sybil bots, organic users, and arbitrage bot wallets without direct financial links? What is the effect of high-signal contracts on the discovery of Sybils, and how robust are behavioral graphs under temporal drift and adversarial perturbations? Our approach synthesizes a symbolic Transaction Grammar from EVM (Ethereum Virtual Machine) traces, capturing separately transaction rhythm, execution structure, and functional intent. The high-signal contracts are filtered with our own protocol, called the Blind-Spot Protocol. Gzip-based NCD is used to construct a behavioral graph for Sybil discovery. We validate this framework against supervised machine learning baselines, a temporal split, and synthetic camouflage stress tests. Ultimately, we contribute a leakage-aware behavioral framework for Sybil candidate discovery. Its core NCD primitive requires no supervised training and can expand suspicious seed wallets without explicit funding links. We position the method as a training-free local discovery primitive for open-world blockchain audits, rather than as a formal open-set recognition system.

</details>


### [126] [Explorative Modeling: Unlocking a Third Pretraining Axis and End-to-End Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27372)
*Alexi Gladstone,Heng Ji,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出探索式建模（Explorative Modeling）新范式，通过分解训练循环而非生成过程，在模型生成与数据之间探索K个候选匹配并选择最优者训练，从而在不分解生成过程的情况下实现端到端生成建模。该方法在图像、视频和语言等多领域展现出随探索规模扩展而持续提升的性能，并在多个效率指标上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习革命表明端到端训练优于手工设计的分阶段方法，但生成模型仍是例外——现有可扩展方法都通过分解生成过程来应对多模态分布，这阻碍了端到端生成。作者希望打破这一局限，让生成模型也能像判别模型一样实现真正的端到端训练。

Method: 探索式建模（XM）的核心是分解训练循环而非生成过程：在训练时探索K个模型生成与数据之间的候选匹配，选择最优匹配进行训练，使预测承诺到具体模式而非模糊化多个模式。这种方法可以作为一种新的预训练轴（探索轴）添加到现有生成模型上，也可独立作为端到端生成建模范式。

Result: 探索带来的收益随规模扩大而增加：数据规模增加时增益从7%提升到36%，模型增长时从13%提升到23%；计算效率提升4.1倍，样本效率提升6.2倍，参数效率提升47%；在ImageNet上达到1.43 FID（近SOTA，无引导）；同时探索式模型在控制任务上以16-256倍更少的推理步骤匹配扩散模型的性能。

Conclusion: 探索式建模既为现有生成模型提供了新的预训练扩展轴，又独立成为一种端到端生成建模新范式，证明分解训练循环比分解生成过程是更好的选择，有望统一生成模型与判别模型的端到端训练理念。

Abstract: The deep learning revolution, kicked off by AlexNet, taught us that end-to-end training beats decomposing a problem into hand-designed stages. Generative modeling, however, has remained the exception-despite generative models being remarkably capable, they are still not trained end-to-end. This is because, at its core, generative modeling is about handling distributions with many modes, and existing scalable approaches handle this the same way, by factoring the generation procedure, which prevents end-to-end generation. In this work, we introduce Explorative Modeling, a new paradigm that instead factors the training loop, exploring K candidate matches between model generations and data, and training on the best, so predictions commit to modes rather than blurring them. We find Explorative Models (XMs) useful in two settings. First, increasing exploration adds a third pretraining axis beyond parameters and data for existing generative models-where scaling exploration monotonically improves performance across both continuous and discrete domains (images, video, and language). Notably, gains from exploration increase with scale, climbing from 7% to 36% as data scales and from 13% to 23% as models grow, with efficiency gains more than doubling at 3x the compute. Concretely, exploration improves FLOP efficiency by 4.1x, sample efficiency by 6.2x, parameter efficiency by 47%, lifts the strongest of image-generation recipes to a near-state-of-the-art 1.43 FID on ImageNet without guidance, enables scaling how end-to-end existing models are, and unlocks scaling generalization. Second, XMs enable end-to-end reconstructive generative modeling, matching diffusion on control tasks with 16-256x fewer inference steps. Together, these results establish XMs as both a new pretraining axis for existing generative models and a standalone end-to-end generative modeling paradigm.

</details>


### [127] [The Convergence Behavior of Adam under Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27383)
*Yijiang Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为普通向量形式的Adam优化器在重尾随机噪声下建立了收敛性保证，将其推广到仅具有有界p阶中心矩的设定，并发现Adam在重尾噪声下收敛到(ρ,ε)-平稳点，但迭代复杂度非最优且依赖于p；当已知域半径并用于控制在线学习器输出时，收敛率可提升至最优复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Adam变体在有限方差的非凸优化中已被证明能达到最优迭代复杂度，但现代深度学习中的随机梯度往往仅具有有界p阶中心矩（p∈(1,2]），即重尾噪声场景，而Adam在此类设定下的行为缺乏理论理解。

Method: 将在线到非凸转化的框架推广到能容纳重尾鞅差噪声的情形，并基于此推广框架为Adam建立折扣遗憾分析（discounted regret analysis），无需限制性的参数耦合假设。

Result: 在重尾噪声下Adam能收敛到(ρ,ε)-平稳点，但迭代复杂度次优且收敛依赖于p，即使在p=2（有限方差）情况下该次优性仍然存在；当域半径已知并用于控制在线学习器输出时，收敛率可提升至最优复杂度。

Conclusion: 该研究为重尾场景下Adam的鲁棒性和局限性提供了新的理论洞见，揭示了Adam在标准设定下的次优性以及利用已知域信息可实现的收敛率改进。

Abstract: We establish the first convergence guarantees for the plain vector-form \emph{Adam} optimizer under heavy-tailed stochastic noise. While several Adam variants are known to achieve optimal iteration complexity in bounded-variance nonconvex optimization, little is understood about their behavior when stochastic gradients admit only a bounded $p$-th central moment for some $p \in (1,2]$, a setting increasingly observed in modern deep learning. To address this gap, we generalize the recent online-to-nonconvex conversion framework to accommodate heavy-tailed martingale-difference noise. Building on this generalized framework, we develop a discounted regret analysis for Adam, without restrictive parameter coupling. Our results show that Adam converges to $(ρ,ε)$-stationary points under heavy-tailed noise. However, it exhibits a suboptimal iteration complexity and $p$-dependent convergence, a suboptimality that persists even in the bounded-variance case ($p=2$). When the domain radius is known and used to control the online-learner output, a standard setup in related literature, the convergence rate improves to match the optimal complexity. These findings provide new theoretical insight into the robustness and limitations of Adam in heavy-tailed regimes.

</details>


### [128] [FunL2O: LLM-Guided Feature Function Design for Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27389)
*Bingheng Li,Junyang Cai,Yupeng Zhang,Bistra Dilkina,Jayant Kalagnanam,Dzung T. Phan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FunL2O，首个通过LLM驱动的程序进化来自动化L2O（learning-to-optimize）特征设计的统一框架，在连续和离散优化任务中均优于手工设计的特征表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习优化（L2O）方法严重依赖手工设计的特征函数将问题实例映射为机器学习模型的输入，特征设计过程繁琐且难以跨领域迁移，缺乏自动化手段。

Method: 采用FunSearch风格的循环框架：LLM提出可执行的特征函数，固定的评估流程重新训练原始L2O模型并度量下游优化性能，通过进化迭代自动发现更好的特征表示。

Result: 在线性规划、二次规划（解预测和热启动）以及混合整数优化（GNN引导的backdoor分支和Predict-and-Search）任务上，跨四种LLM，进化出的特征一致优于手工设计的表示。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的特征进化是L2O中自动化表示设计的一种通用且有效的方法，弥补了L2O管道中特征设计环节的空白。

Abstract: Learning-to-optimize (L2O) methods accelerate repeated optimization by training models to predict solutions, warm starts, branching decisions, or other forms of solver guidance. A critical yet largely overlooked component of these pipelines is the feature function that maps problem instances to inputs for machine learning models. Existing L2O methods typically rely on hand-crafted features, making representation design manual and largely fixed across domains. We introduce FunL2O, the first unified framework for automating feature design through LLM-driven program evolution for L2O. In a FunSearch-style loop, an LLM proposes executable feature functions, while a fixed evaluation process retrains the original L2O model and measures downstream optimization performance. We evaluate FunL2O on linear and quadratic programming tasks involving solution prediction and warm-starting, as well as on mixed-integer optimization tasks using GNN-guided backdoor branching and Predict-and-Search. Across continuous and discrete optimization tasks and four LLMs, the evolved features consistently outperform hand-crafted representations. These results establish LLM-driven feature evolution as a general and effective approach to automating representation design in L2O.

</details>


### [129] [ECG-InterpBench: Benchmarking the Interpretability of ECG Foundation Models with Matched-Scale Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27404)
*Yixuan Duan,Wei Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ECG-InterpBench，一个用于系统评估心电图基础模型表征可解释性的基准测试。该基准使用稀疏自编码器作为标准化测量工具，在容量匹配条件下对六个冻结的心电图基础模型进行450个单元的全面评估，涵盖稀疏重建保真度、49项临床测量指标的覆盖度以及跨随机种子的特征可重复性等多个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心电图基础模型基准主要评估下游预测性能，无法深入揭示模型内部表征是否可以被忠实分解、临床解释或在独立分析中复现。缺乏一个系统性的框架来评估表征可解释性。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器作为标准化测量仪器，在五个标准编码器深度、五个匹配字典宽度和三个随机种子下评估六个冻结的心电图基础模型，构建450单元的"可解释性图谱"，并从稀疏重建保真度、单特征可访问性与覆盖度（49项临床心电图测量）以及跨种子特征可重复性三个维度进行评估，同时量化患者采样不确定性、深度/种子变化以及对稀疏参数化的敏感性。

Result: 实验发现心电图基础模型展现出不同的可解释性特征。在MIMIC-IV-ECG上的匹配复现实验证实，重建保真度和临床可访问性会识别出不同的领先模型，表明这两个维度并非等价。

Conclusion: ECG-InterpBench补充了以性能为中心的ECG基准测试，提供了容量可控且可复现的框架，用于跨不同表征可解释性维度比较心电图基础模型。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for electrocardiogram foundation models primarily evaluate downstream predictive performance, providing limited insight into whether their internal representations can be faithfully decomposed, clinically interpreted, or reproduced across independent analyses. We introduce ECG-InterpBench, a benchmark designed to systematically evaluate the interpretability of ECG foundation-model representations. ECG-InterpBench uses sparse autoencoders as standardized measurement instruments and matches their capacity across models to enable controlled comparisons. We evaluate six frozen ECG foundation models across five standardized encoder depths, five matched dictionary widths, and three random seeds, producing a 450-cell interpretability atlas comprising 75 exactly matched six-model comparison blocks. The benchmark evaluates complementary dimensions of representation interpretability, including sparse reconstruction fidelity, single-feature accessibility and coverage of 49 clinically meaningful ECG measurements, and cross-seed feature reproducibility. The evaluation further quantifies patient-sampling uncertainty, depth- and seed-dependent variation, and sensitivity to the sparsity parameterization. The benchmark reveals that ECG foundation models exhibit distinct interpretability profiles. A matched replication on MIMIC-IV-ECG confirms that reconstruction fidelity and clinical accessibility identify different leading models. The benchmark is accompanied by executable evaluation code, standardized manifests, cell-level metrics, and reproducibility audits. ECG-InterpBench complements performance-centered ECG benchmarks by providing a capacity-controlled and reproducible framework for comparing ECG foundation models across distinct dimensions of representation interpretability.

</details>


### [130] [Context-Informed Ship Trajectory Prediction via Conditional Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27418)
*Yuan Guan,Chandler Squires,Timothy Hu,Pradeep Ravikumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Conditional Informer的新型编码器-解码器架构，将船舶轨迹预测建模为条件生成任务，通过条件注意力机制显式编码天气等环境因素对船舶动态的单向物理依赖关系，并引入模态掩蔽训练策略应对传感器数据缺失场景。在AIS和ERA5数据上的实验表明，该方法在上下文可用时预测准确率提升15.4%，且在传感器回退场景下将误差降低近一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的船舶轨迹预测方法主要依赖历史运动学状态，将船舶运动视为孤立系统，忽略了天气等外在因素和静态船舶特性的影响。已有的多模态方法将环境变量作为平行特征，未能建模环境条件对船舶动态的单向物理因果依赖，因此预测能力受限。

Method: 提出Conditional Informer架构，其核心是条件注意力机制，使船舶状态通过交叉注意力显式查询环境上下文，编码"天气调节但不由船舶生成"的物理先验；同时引入模态掩蔽（Modality Masking）训练策略，模拟传感器数据缺失场景，防止模型在回退时发生灾难性退化。

Result: 在AIS和ERA5数据的实验中，该方法在上下文可用时比运动学基线及拼接式多模态基线预测准确率提升15.4%；模态掩蔽策略有效防止捷径学习，在传感器回退场景下将误差降低近一个数量级。

Conclusion: 通过显式编码环境对船舶动态的方向性物理依赖关系，并采用模态掩蔽训练策略增强鲁棒性，Conditional Informer在长期轨迹预测准确性和传感器缺失情况下的鲁棒性方面均显著优于现有方法，验证了条件生成范式在船舶轨迹预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Long-term ship trajectory prediction is a fundamental capability for maritime safety and autonomous navigation. While recent Transformer-based architectures have improved forecasting horizons, they predominantly rely on historical kinematic states, treating vessel motion as an isolated system. In reality, maritime navigation is profoundly modulated by extrinsic factors like weather and constrained by static vessel characteristics. Existing multimodal approaches fundamentally model the joint distribution over states and contexts, treating environmental variables as peer features rather than encoding the directional physical dependence of vessel dynamics on environmental conditions.
  In this work, we propose the Conditional Informer, a novel encoder-decoder architecture that formulates trajectory prediction as a conditional generation task. We employ a dedicated Conditional Attention mechanism where the vessel state explicitly queries environmental contexts through cross-attention, encoding the physical prior that weather modulates - but is not generated by - vessel dynamics. Furthermore, to address the intermittency of real-world data, we introduce a Modality Masking training strategy to prevent catastrophic degradation during sensor fallback. Extensive experiments on AIS and ERA5 data demonstrate that our approach outperforms kinematic and concatenation-based baselines by 15.4% in prediction accuracy when context is available. Crucially, Modality Masking prevents shortcut learning, reducing fallback error by nearly an order of magnitude compared to unconstrained models.

</details>


### [131] [Good Rankers, Bad Objectives: Bilinear Contrastive Critics under Expressive Policy Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27422)
*Ayushman Singh,Siddharth Aphale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了对比评论家（contrastive critic）在动作排序中的安全性问题，发现即使排序良好，其分数也不适合作为最大化目标用于best-of-K选择、规划等任务。通过受控支持分解，作者将原始双线性分数的后悔归因于范数漂移，并发现余弦约束和混合方法仍无法避免选择支持外的动作。对比分数在四个OGBench导航任务中弱校准或倒置，而Bellman训练的TD-Q方法表现成功。训练/读取分解揭示了排序丢失源于余弦训练目标，原始训练的嵌入在推断期归一化后仍保留弱排序能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比评论家被广泛用作价值类目标进行动作选择，但目前对其作为最大化目标的安全性缺乏深入研究。作者旨在揭示即使对比评论家在排序指标上表现良好，其在作为选择/规划目标时可能因支持外值膨胀、范数漂移等问题导致失败，从而提醒社区注意这类方法的局限性。

Method: 作者设计了受控支持分解方法将原始双线性后悔归因于范数漂移；在四个OGBench导航任务中评估对比分数在最高分十分位上的校准性；采用参数匹配的函数类控制实验比较对比方法与Bellman训练的TD-Q；通过训练/读取分解区分训练目标和推断期归一化的影响；使用模拟器展开（rollouts）量化实际选择成本。

Result: 1) 原始双线性分数的后悔主要来自范数漂移，余弦约束无法消除失败；2) 对比和混合评论家在多数动作池中选择支持外动作且后悔相当；3) 对比分数在四个导航任务的最高分十分位中弱校准或倒置，无法按值排序固定查询动作；4) Bellman训练的TD-Q成功排序；5) 实际成本因任务而异——PointMaze和精确Q*玩具上有单步选择成本，而AntMaze和HumanoidMaze上控制器可自我纠正；6) 训练/读取分解显示排序丢失源于余弦训练目标，原始训练嵌入在推断期归一化后保留弱排序。

Conclusion: 对比评论家作为兼容性排序器在导航和操作任务中仍然有用，但动作选择需要价值校准的标量目标。候选最大化可能利用范数漂移、分数饱和或支持内误排产生的假阳性。研究表明对比分数不应用于安全的价值最大化场景，而应改用Bellman训练的TD-Q等方法。

Abstract: Good action rankings do not make a contrastive critic safe to maximize. These critics increasingly act as value-like objectives for best-of-$K$ selection, planning, and critic-guided generation. Unbounded bilinear scores can let large embedding norms inflate off-support values, but cosine bounding does not remove the failure. A controlled support decomposition attributes most raw bilinear regret to norm drift. Cosine and hybrid critics nevertheless select off-support actions from most pools and incur comparable regret. Contrastive scores are weakly calibrated or inverted in the top score decile across four OGBench navigation tasks, and they fail to order fixed-query actions by value. Bellman-trained TD-Q succeeds, including in a parameter-matched function-class control. Realized costs depend on the task: simulator rollouts reveal single-step selection costs on PointMaze and the exact-$Q^*$ toy but well-powered nulls on AntMaze and HumanoidMaze, where the controller can self-correct. A training/readout decomposition traces the lost ordering to the cosine training objective; raw-trained embeddings retain weak ordering after inference-time normalization. Candidate maximization can therefore exploit false positives caused by norm drift, score saturation, or in-support misranking. Contrastive critics remain useful compatibility rankers on navigation and manipulation tasks, but action selection requires a value-calibrated scalar.

</details>


### [132] [SE(3)-MeanFlow: Few-Step Protein Backbone Generation on Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27431)
*Yikun Bai,Binghang Lu,Yikai Liu,Elaheh Akbari,Soheil Kolouri,Linxuan Wang,Ping He,Shuchan Wang,Ruqi Zhang,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SE(3)-MeanFlow，一种用于蛋白质骨架生成的少步生成框架，将MeanFlow从欧几里得空间扩展至蛋白质帧的李群几何，通过闭式平均速度恒等式实现无模拟训练，并引入SE(3) alpha-Flow目标作为预热阶段，在少步采样条件下匹配或超越流匹配基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的蛋白质骨架生成扩散模型和流匹配模型虽然能在SE(3)^N上生成高质量骨架，但推理时需要对ODE进行数百次数值积分，每次涉及李群指数映射计算，成为高通量设计流程的瓶颈。亟需一种少步生成框架来突破这一效率瓶颈。

Method: 在so(3)李代数和R^3中推导旋转和平移的闭式平均速度恒等式，提供无模拟训练目标；引入SE(3) alpha-Flow目标以消除旋转支路的雅可比-向量积，并将其作为预热阶段，随后切换至小时间步稳定的MeanFlow损失用于预训练剩余阶段和基于rectification的后训练。

Result: 在蛋白质骨架生成任务中，SE(3)-MeanFlow在匹配或超过使用数倍采样步数的流匹配基线的同时，在少步采样场景下优势更加明显；通过rectification后训练，在每个采样预算下均领先基线，仅以略微降低的多样性为代价。

Conclusion: SE(3)-MeanFlow证明了将MeanFlow思想扩展到蛋白质帧的李群几何是可行且有效的，为高通量蛋白质设计提供了更高效的少步生成方案，在采样效率与生成质量之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Generative modeling of protein backbones promises the de novo design of proteins with prescribed structural and functional properties. Existing diffusion and flow-matching models produce high-quality backbones on SE(3)^N, but inference requires numerically integrating an ODE over hundreds of network evaluations, each involving a Lie group exponential map - a bottleneck for high-throughput design campaigns. We introduce SE(3)-MeanFlow, a few-step generative framework that extends MeanFlow from Euclidean space to the Lie group geometry of protein frames. Working natively in the Lie algebra so(3) and in R^3, we derive closed-form average-velocity identities for rotations and translations, giving simulation-free training targets. We further introduce an SE(3) alpha-Flow objective that removes the Jacobian-vector product from the rotation branch and serves as a warm-up stage, after which training switches to a small-t stabilized MeanFlow loss that is used for the remainder of pretraining and for rectification-based post-training. In protein backbone generation, SE(3)-MeanFlow matches or exceeds flow-matching baselines that use several times more sampling steps, and its advantage widens in the few-step regime, where rectification lets it lead at every matched budget - at a modest cost in diversity.

</details>


### [133] [Neural Network-Assisted CLEAN for Channel Modeling in Low-SNR Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27450)
*Chaofan Deng,Linyu Sun,Jaeho Lee,Arijit Raychowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NN-CLEAN混合框架，将多头残差网络嵌入CLEAN迭代提取循环中，用快速前向传播替代穷举网格搜索，同时保留精确的数学残差减法，在低信噪比环境下实现高效且高精度的多径参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最大似然估计算法（如CLEAN）精度高但计算复杂度极高（穷举网格搜索）；纯数据驱动的深度学习方法缺乏物理基础，难以在可变多径密度和离网格参数下泛化。两者均无法满足现代无线通信系统在低信噪比环境下的实时高精度多径估计需求。

Method: 提出Neural Network-Assisted CLEAN（NN-CLEAN）混合框架：将多头残差网络嵌入CLEAN迭代提取循环中，用快速可并行化的前向传播取代穷举网格搜索，将残差减法交由精确数学模型处理，从而避免非物理误差的累积并精确提取物理多径参数。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真表明：在5dB SNR下，NN-CLEAN估计精度超过96%，与传统的Grid-Search CLEAN基线匹配；计算复杂度大幅降低，明显优于子空间方法和独立单次神经网络；随批量增大，执行时间和内存消耗呈近似平坦的扩展，展现出高度并行化效率。

Conclusion: NN-CLEAN兼具高精度和高效率，其高度并行化和近线性的资源扩展特性使其成为MIMO系统信道估计的鲁棒实时解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate multipath parameter estimation is critical for modern wireless communication systems, particularly in challenging low-SNR environments. Traditional Maximum Likelihood Estimation algorithms, such as CLEAN, provide high-resolution parameter extraction but suffer from prohibitive computational complexity due to exhaustive grid search. Conversely, purely data-driven deep learning approaches lack physical grounding and struggle to generalize across variable multipath densities and off-grid parameters. To address these limitations, this paper proposes Neural Network-Assisted CLEAN (NN-CLEAN), a hybrid framework that embeds a multi-head residual network directly into the iterative CLEAN extraction loop. By replacing the exhaustive grid search with rapid, parallelizable forward passes while delegating residual subtraction to exact mathematical models, NN-CLEAN isolates physical multipath parameters without accumulating non- physical errors. Extensive Monte Carlo simulations demonstrate that NN-CLEAN achieves estimation accuracy exceeding 96% at 5 dB SNR, matching the traditional Grid-Search CLEAN (GS- CLEAN) baseline, while providing a massive reduction in computational complexity and substantially outperforming subspace methods and standalone one-shot neural networks. Crucially, NN-CLEAN exhibits a near-flat scaling in execution runtime and memory consumption as batch sizes increase. This highly efficient parallelization establishes NN-CLEAN as a robust, real- time solution for channel estimation in MIMO systems.

</details>


### [134] [FADEx: Feature Attribution and Distortion-based Explanation of Dimensionality Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27463)
*Lucas Greff Meneses,Evandro S. Ortigossa,Claudio Silva,Luis Gustavo Nonato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FADEx，一种基于一阶泰勒展开和奇异值分解的局部逐实例特征归因方法，用于解释非线性降维方法的投影结果。FADEx通过加权最小二乘法计算局部线性模型，无需样本外数据映射，与具体DR方法无关，可同时提供局部特征归因和失真分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性降维技术本身是不透明变换，难以理解单个特征如何影响实例在降维空间中的位置。现有DR解释方法存在每个特征多个归因和对特定DR方法适用性受限等局限。

Method: FADEx采用一阶泰勒展开进行局部线性逼近，结合奇异值分解提供解释；通过加权最小二乘法计算局部线性模型，无需样本外数据映射，从而对DR方法具有不可知性（agnostic），同时提供局部特征归因和失真分析。

Result: 通过定性和定量评估、与现有方法的比较以及案例研究，FADEx在提供解释和DR行为分析资源方面表现出有效性和通用性，解释稳健可靠，在多个方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: FADEx是一种有效的、通用的局部特征归因方法，能够为非线性降维方法提供稳健可靠的解释，并且不受具体DR方法的限制。

Abstract: Dimensionality Reduction (DR) is a fundamental tool for high-dimensional data exploration, reducing the complexity of latent spaces of machine learning models, and assisting in the explanation of complex opaque models. However, non-linear DR techniques often function as opaque transformations themselves, making it challenging to understand how individual features influence instance positioning in the reduced space. This lack of transparency complicates the analysis and interpretation of structural patterns, hindering the ability to reason about the organization of high-dimensional data based on the projected layout. In order to address this challenge, dimensionality reduction explanation methods have shown promise in improving the understanding of the observed groups and cluster structures. Unfortunately, existing DR explanation approaches tend to suffer from limitations such as multiple attributions per feature and restricted applicability to specific dimensionality reduction methods, which hinder their use. In this work, we propose FADEx, a novel local per-instance feature attribution method that leverages local linear approximation via first-order Taylor expansion and Singular Value Decomposition to provide explanations. FADEx computes the local linear models via weighted least squares, eliminating the need for out-of-sample data mapping, making it agnostic to the DR method, while simultaneously providing local feature attributions and distortion analysis.
