Our results show that Topoformer matches or exceeds strong GNN and topology-based baselines while offering predictable and efficient compute. This work opens a new path for parallelizable and unifying approaches to graph representation learning that integrate topological inductive biases into attention frameworks.

</details>


### [203] [HARGO: Heterogeneity-Aware Reward-Guided Optimization for RL Post-Training of LLMs on HPC Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28301)
*Tiangang Li,Xiangbo Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HARGO（异质性感知奖励引导优化）方法，通过置信度调制的优势函数实现逐响应重要性加权，无需任务类型标签即可有效对齐异构HPC任务，在四个任务和九种方法中全面取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调（SFT）虽然能为LLM注入HPC领域知识，但仅靠知识无法保证任务适当的行为——同一模型在不同HPC任务（如二分类、事实问答、语义生成）上表现出极大的异质性（答案长度差异58倍、三种不同的奖励分布、SFT精度差异大），使得GRPO等统一权重的RL方法表现次优。

Method: HARGO通过两个信号计算逐响应重要性权重：(1) 从组级奖励对比中提取判别信号（discrimination signal）；(2) 从参考模型对数概率中提取置信信号（confidence signal），两者共同调制优势函数后再计算逐响应权重，整个过程无需任务类型标签。

Result: 在四个HPC任务和九种方法的对比中，HARGO在所有三个主要指标上取得最优：WinRate 54.62%、Data Race F1 91.30%、PLP相似度0.8558。消融实验确认两个信号具有互补贡献。

Conclusion: HARGO在异构HPC任务上建立了最佳的整体对齐质量，无需任务类型标签即可自适应地处理不同任务之间的巨大差异性，验证了置信度调制优势加权在异构场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) can equip large language models (LLMs) with domain knowledge for high-performance computing (HPC) tasks such as data race detection and benchmark question answering. However, knowledge alone does not guarantee task-appropriate behavior: the same SFT model that correctly classifies 88.65\% of C/C++ data race samples produces verbose, imprecise answers to factual queries, with 65.9\% of MLPerf responses exceeding 40 characters. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training addresses this gap by optimizing for task-specific rewards rather than token-level imitation. Yet HPC tasks exhibit extreme heterogeneity, with binary classification, factual QA, and semantic generation differing by 58x in answer length, spanning three distinct reward distributions, and showing widely varying SFT accuracy. This makes uniform-weight RL methods such as GRPO suboptimal. We propose HARGO, Heterogeneity-Aware Reward-Guided Optimization, which introduces per-response importance weighting via confidence-modulated advantage: computing a discrimination signal from group-level reward contrast and a confidence signal from reference model log-probabilities, then modulating the advantage before computing per-response weights, without requiring task-type labels. Across four HPC tasks and nine methods, HARGO achieves the best performance on all three primary metrics: WinRate 54.62\%, Data Race F1 91.30\%, and PLP Similarity 0.8558. Ablation confirms complementary contributions from both signals. HARGO establishes the best overall alignment quality among compared methods for heterogeneous HPC tasks.

</details>


### [204] [Semi-Supervised Learning for Molecular Graphs via Ensemble Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28304)
*Rasmus Tirsgaard,Laurits Fredsgaard,Marisa Wodrich,Mikkel Jordahn,Mikkel N. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用集成共识（ensemble consensus）的半监督学习方法，在分子科学领域无需标签保持增强即可提升图神经网络预测精度，且单个集成成员性能优于传统全监督训练的完整集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子科学中标记数据获取成本高且耗时，而大量未标记分子数据容易获得。传统的半监督学习方法依赖标签保持的数据增强，但在分子领域微小的结构变化就可能导致性质剧变，因此很难设计合适的增强策略。

Method: 采用基于集成共识的半监督学习目标函数训练图神经网络，无需设计标签保持的数据增强。该方法在多种分子数据集、任务类型和图神经网络架构上进行验证。

Result: 集成共识目标函数训练可提升模型鲁棒性，并产生类似知识蒸馏的效果。单个集成成员在几乎所有情况下优于传统监督学习训练出的完整集成，同时降低了校准误差。

Conclusion: 集成共识的半监督方法是分子科学领域中一种有效的、无需数据增强的替代方案，不仅提升了预测准确性和鲁棒性，还改善了模型校准。

Abstract: Machine learning is transforming molecular sciences by accelerating property prediction, simulation, and the discovery of new molecules and materials. Acquiring labeled data in these domains is often costly and time-consuming, whereas large collections of unlabeled molecular data are readily available. Standard semi-supervised learning methods often rely on label-preserving augmentations, which are challenging to design in the molecular domain, where minor changes can drastically alter properties. In this work, we show that semi-supervised methods that rely on an ensemble consensus can boost predictive accuracy across a diverse range of molecular datasets, task types, and graph neural network architectures. We find that training with an ensemble consensus objective increases robustness in models and exhibits an effect similar to knowledge distillation; an individual member of an ensemble trained this way outperforms a full ensemble trained in a traditional supervised fashion in almost all cases. In addition, this type of semi-supervised training reduces calibration error.

</details>


### [205] [Beyond Geometric Complementarity: Coherent Overlap in Sparse Mixture-of-Experts Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28308)
*Huiyuan Tian,Bonan Xu,Shijian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"相干重叠"（coherent overlap）概念，区分了稀疏MoE语言模型中路由一致性、候选质量和候选-上下文交互三个量。通过专家子空间分离指数（ESSI）、匹配路由残差和前缀控制的2×2析因设计，发现专家子空间高度重叠但实际路由仍优于匹配替代方案，且路由选择虽在几何上被前缀压缩，但在功能上仍有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于稀疏MoE语言模型的研究常将路由一致性、候选质量和候选-上下文交互三个概念混淆，导致对"共同选择的专家应贡献不同表征方向"这一几何解释的证据不够清晰。作者希望解开这些混淆的量，以明确几何相似性本身是否能决定冗余或剪枝价值。

Method: 提出三个分析工具与实验设计：(1) 专家子空间分离指数（ESSI）度量子空间重叠程度；(2) 匹配路由残差比较实际路由与匹配替代方案；(3) 前缀控制的2×2析因设计分离候选质量与上下文交互。同时使用冻结路由干预和受控Top-k训练研究评估功能价值。实验覆盖6种MoE架构及OLMoE、Mixtral、DeepSeek中的39个析因单元格。

Result: (1) 六种MoE架构中专家子空间高度重叠，但实际路由解释token表征优于匹配替代方案；(2) 全部39个单元格中，所选候选解释的残差表征超过最强未选中对手，但实际前缀在所有单元格中均缩小了这一优势，所有交互均为负且95%置信区间低于零；(3) 几何缩小不意味着功能冗余：39个冻结路由比较中24个显示添加后期专家改善下一token预测，其余15个不具结论性；受控训练研究中Top-2在三个种子中均优于Top-1。

Conclusion: 作者提出"相干重叠"这一联合模式：路由从共享几何邻域中选择与token相关的专家，同时有用的多专家计算在没有不相交线性覆盖的情况下持续存在。将上述三个量分离后可见，几何相似性本身不能单独决定冗余或剪枝价值，这对MoE模型的高效部署和压缩策略具有重要启示。

Abstract: Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) language models route each token to multiple experts, suggesting a geometric account of their benefit: co-selected experts should contribute distinct representation directions. Existing evidence often conflates route coherence, candidate quality, and candidate-by-context interaction. We distinguish these quantities using an Expert Subspace Separation Index (ESSI), matched-route residuals, and a prefix-controlled $2\times2$ factorial; frozen-route interventions and a controlled Top-$k$ study assess functional value. Three paired contrasts organize the findings. First, across six MoE architectures, expert subspaces overlap substantially, yet actual routes explain token representations better than matched alternatives. Second, across the 39 factorial cells in OLMoE, Mixtral, and DeepSeek, the selected candidate explains more of the residual representation than the strongest unselected rival in every cell, yet the actual prefix narrows this advantage throughout: all interactions are negative, and every 95% confidence interval lies below zero. Third, this geometric narrowing does not imply functional redundancy: adding later experts improves next-token prediction in 24 of 39 frozen-route comparisons, while the other 15 estimates are inconclusive; a controlled training study also favors Top-2 over Top-1 in all three seeds. We call this joint pattern coherent overlap: routing selects token-relevant experts from a shared geometric neighborhood, while useful multi-expert computation persists without disjoint linear coverage. Separating these quantities clarifies why geometric similarity alone cannot determine redundancy or pruning value.

</details>


### [206] [Measuring Distortion in the Empty Regions of Dimensionality Reduction Scatterplots with the Gap Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28324)
*Jaume Ros,Alessio Arleo,Fernando Paulovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Gap Index (GI)质量度量指标，通过将2D投影空间分解为空三角形并与其高维对应进行变形比较，来捕捉投影中空白区域的空间变形，弥补现有质量指标仅关注点间直接关系而忽视空白区域视觉失真的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流降维投影质量指标主要聚焦于点与点之间的直接关系（如距离或邻域），忽略了投影布局中空白区域的变形。然而，空白区域往往构成2D布局的视觉重要特征，这种忽视会导致对投影质量的评估不完整。

Method: 提出Gap Index (GI)质量度量：将投影空间分解为空三角形，将其与高维空间中的对应三角形进行对比，计算每个三角形的变形程度。变形值可聚合为单一标量用于整体评估，也可叠加在投影上可视化区域性的失真模式。

Result: 与主流质量指标相比，GI对具有高视觉冲击力的小型结构变形更为敏感，同时计算速度快且结果易于解释。

Conclusion: GI作为一种新颖的质量度量指标，有效填补了现有指标在空白区域失真评估方面的空白，为高维数据投影的视觉分析提供了更全面的质量评估工具。

Abstract: Quality metrics play a crucial role in the proper use of dimensionality reduction projections for visual analysis of high-dimensional data. They quantify the degree of distortion of a projection compared to the high-dimensional data and provide a reliable indication of how confident users can be in the structures they see in the resulting layouts. However, most popular metrics focus on capturing direct relationships between points (e.g., distances or neighborhoods) while neglecting distortions in empty areas of the layout, even though these often compose visually relevant features of a 2D layout. In this paper, we introduce the Gap Index (GI), a quality metric for 2D projections that captures visual distortion by measuring spatial distortion in empty areas of a projection. It does so by decomposing the space into empty triangles, which are then compared to their high-dimensional counterparts to compute the deformation. This per-triangle deformation can be aggregated into a single scalar value or overlaid on a projection to visualize regional distortion patterns. Results show that, contrary to popular quality metrics, the GI is sensitive to small structural deformations that have high visual impact. It is also fast to compute and interpretable.

</details>


### [207] [Encryption-Compatible Clustered Federated Learning via Distributed Expectation-Maximization over Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28338)
*Michael Ben Ali,Imen Megdiche,André Péninou,Olivier Teste*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FLAMECHE方法，将基于元数据的聚类联邦学习（CFL）重新表述为分布式期望最大化（EM）过程，通过限制服务器仅进行加法操作，使其兼容安全的联邦学习加密方案，从而在隐私保护、通信成本和计算效率三难困境中取得更好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CFL方法在隐私保护、通信成本和计算效率之间面临权衡（即CFL三难困境）。基于元数据的聚类方法虽然通信和计算高效，但与标准FL隐私保护机制不兼容，这是一个关键局限性。

Method: FLAMECHE将元数据驱动的CFL形式化为分布式期望最大化（EM）程序，限制服务器端更新仅使用加法操作，从而保证与实用安全FL加密方案的兼容性，同时保持聚类效率。

Result: 在多个数据集和多种异质性场景下的大量实验表明，FLAMECHE能够提升客户端模型的有效性，并实现加密兼容的元数据聚类，改善了其在CFL三难困境中的定位。

Conclusion: FLAMECHE成功解决了元数据聚类方法与隐私保护机制不兼容的问题，为CFL三难困境提供了一种更优的平衡方案，使通信高效和计算高效的同时不牺牲隐私安全。

Abstract: Clustered Federated Learning (CFL) addresses data heterogeneity in federated settings by grouping clients with similar data distributions to enable effective training. Existing methods face a trade-off between privacy preservation, communication cost, and computational efficiency. We formalize this as the CFL trilemma, according to which improving two of these dimensions comes at the expense of the third. A prominent paradigm relies on metadata (i.e., low-dimensional representations of client datasets shared with the server) to enable communication- and computation-efficient clustering. However, such approaches are not compatible with standard FL privacy-preserving mechanisms. To address this limitation, we propose FLAMECHE, which reformulates metadata-based CFL as a distributed Expectation-Maximization (EM) procedure, restricting server updates to additive operations while preserving efficiency. This design enables compatibility with practical secure FL schemes. We conducted extensive experiments on multiple datasets under various heterogeneous scenarios. Results show that FLAMECHE improves the effectiveness of client models. It enables encryption-compatible metadata-based clustering, enhancing its positioning within the CFL trilemma.

</details>


### [208] [LEDGERMIND: Provenance-Constrained Multimodal Agentic Reasoning with a Structured Evidence Ledger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28374)
*Enjun Du,Hange Zhou,Chenxu Du,Siyi Liu,Zirong Chen,Ziyu Zheng,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LedgerMind框架，将多模态智能体轨迹建模为受溯源约束的状态机，通过结构化证据账本、三层接地协议和自适应调度器等机制，同时提升答案准确性和轨迹级忠实度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态智能体评估仅关注最终答案准确率，无法区分正确答案是来自可靠的证据基础还是语言先验或偶然的错误抵消。作者希望提供一种能保证推理过程可溯源、可验证的机制，识别并修复四个易被最终准确率掩盖的失败模式。

Method: 将多模态智能体轨迹视为溯源约束的状态机：工具输出归一化为结构化证据账本作为轨迹状态；下游推理只能引用活跃账本条目；在实体和数值层面检查接地；通过类型化状态转换实现修复且不能引入无溯源内容。结合三层接地协议、自适应双路径调度器和事件触发验证修复引擎（带形式化非放大保证）。

Result: 在多个多模态推理基准和骨干MLLM上验证，LedgerMind同时提升了答案准确性和轨迹级忠实度。

Conclusion: 通过将溯源约束嵌入智能体推理流程，可以同时改善最终答案的质量和中间推理过程的可信度，为多模态智能体评估提供超越单一准确率指标的更全面方案。

Abstract: Multimodal agents for visual question answering increasingly operate as multi-step trajectories that interleave perception, retrieval, and reasoning, yet evaluation still largely reduces to final-answer accuracy. This aggregate signal cannot tell whether a correct answer was reached through grounded evidence, language priors, or accidental error cancellation. We propose to treat a multimodal agent trajectory as a provenance-constrained state machine: tool outputs are normalized into a Structured Evidence Ledger that serves as the trajectory state, downstream reasoning and decision claims may cite only active ledger entries, grounding is checked at the entity and numeric level, and repair is realized as typed state transitions that cannot introduce content without tool-produced provenance. We instantiate this design as LedgerMind (Provenance-Constrained Multimodal Agentic Reasoning with a Structured Evidence Ledger), augmented by a Three-Layer Grounding Protocol, an Adaptive Dual-Path Dispatcher that matches reasoning depth to question complexity, and an Event-Triggered Verification-and-Repair engine with a formal provenance non-amplification guarantee. We use LedgerMind to target four recurring failure patterns that final-answer accuracy tends to obscure: unsupported intermediate reasoning, citation-backed entity hallucination (Phantom Grounding), over-reasoning on simple queries, and repair-time amplification. Experiments across multiple multimodal reasoning benchmarks and backbone MLLMs show that LedgerMind improves both answer accuracy and trajectory-level faithfulness.

</details>


### [209] [Hierarchical Multilevel Monte Carlo for Order-Optimal Neural Actor-Critic in Average-Reward CMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28390)
*Ankur Naskar,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于层次化多级蒙特卡洛（MLMC）神经评论家估计器的原始-对偶自然Actor-Critic算法，首次在无限时域平均奖励约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）中，实现了具有通用策略参数化和神经评论家的阶最优收敛保证（最优性差距和约束违例均为O(T^{-1/2})），且无需预先知道混合时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMDP是安全关键强化学习的自然框架，但现有的primal-dual actor-critic方法在神经评论家设定下无法获得阶最优收敛保证。核心瓶颈在于神经评论家估计中存在根本性的偏差-成本权衡：在NTK分析下，降低评论家偏差会大幅增加优化成本，阻碍原始-对偶框架中的阶最优收敛。这一开放问题需要解决。

Method: 论文引入了一个层次化的多级蒙特卡洛（MLMC）神经评论家估计器，同时在轨迹采样和评论家优化两个维度上进行去偏。该估计器以仅对数级别的期望样本成本，获得与长评论家优化运行相当的偏差。基于此估计器，构建了原始-对偶自然Actor-Critic算法。

Result: 所提出的算法实现了最优性差距和约束违例均为O(T^{-1/2})阶的收敛保证，这是无限时域平均奖励CMDP下第一个阶最优收敛结果，适用于通用策略参数化和神经评论家，且无需知道底层混合时间。即使在没有约束的设定下，该结果也是新颖的。

Conclusion: 本文通过MLMC神经评论家估计器解决了神经评论家估计中的偏差-成本权衡难题，首次在通用参数化下的平均奖励CMDP中建立了阶最优收敛保证，为安全关键强化学习提供了理论支撑，且消除了对混合时间先验知识的需求。

Abstract: Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDPs) provide a natural framework for reinforcement learning in safety-critical applications, where agents maximize long-term reward while satisfying long-term constraints. Although primal-dual actor-critic methods with linear critics are well understood, extending order-optimal convergence guarantees to neural critics in average-reward CMDPs has remained open. The main challenge is a fundamental bias-cost trade-off in neural critic estimation: under Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) analysis, reducing critic bias substantially increases critic optimization cost, preventing order-optimal convergence in the primal-dual framework. We resolve this bottleneck by introducing a hierarchical Multilevel Monte Carlo (MLMC) neural critic that performs debiasing simultaneously across trajectory sampling and critic optimization. The resulting estimator attains the bias of a long critic optimization run with only logarithmic expected sample cost. Building on this estimator, we develop a primal-dual Natural Actor-Critic algorithm that achieves both an optimality gap and a constraint violation of order $\tilde{O}(T^{-1/2})$. This establishes the first order-optimal convergence guarantees for infinite-horizon average-reward CMDPs with general policy parameterization and neural critics, while eliminating the need to know the underlying mixing time. Our results are novel even in the unconstrained setting.

</details>


### [210] [Why Are GUI Agents Correct but Late? Decode on the Decision-Time Critical Path, Tested with Pre-Compiled Policy Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28399)
*Zihan Dong,Rui Qian,Qishi Zhan,Dongshen Peng,Kaixin Li,Yu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应预期策略树（AAPT），通过在空闲屏幕期间预先构建带条件守卫的策略树，消除计算机使用代理在决策关键路径上的自回归解码延迟，将瞬时GUI事件处理成功率从0.50提升至0.79。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用代理常因瞬时GUI事件失败，因为产生正确动作时相关窗口已经关闭。根本原因是昂贵的自回归解码位于决策时间的关键路径上。

Method: AAPT利用空闲屏幕时段，让相同的冻结多模态模型构建一个有界的条件策略树，包含可观察守卫、预授权动作和分支级截止时间。树的大小覆盖模型自身的解码延迟。事件发生时，轻量级观察者将变化门控帧与已准备的分支匹配，并立即执行对应动作，无需生成新文本。

Result: 配对试验中AAPT将成功率从0.50提升至0.79（p=1.8×10^-3），且无错误动作。开环和预测重规划基线成功率为零。在独立通用多模态模型上126对试验中效果复现（p=4.9×10^-13）。外部基准上AAPT与反应式基线整体性能相当，但两者优势互补。

Conclusion: AAPT在候选动作可预先枚举时表现最佳，而当动作无法预先枚举时反应式执行更强。前置注册的oracle探针否定了初始假设，指出分支路由是因果瓶颈。

Abstract: Computer-use agents often fail on transient GUI events because they produce the correct action only after the relevant window has already closed. We identify the main cause as expensive autoregressive decoding on the decision-time critical path. We propose Adaptive Anticipatory Policy Trees (AAPT), which eliminates this delay without modifying the underlying model. During idle screen periods, the same frozen multimodal model constructs a bounded conditional policy tree with observable guards, pre-authorized actions, and branch-specific deadlines. The tree is sized to cover the model's own decoding latency. When an event occurs, a lightweight observer matches change-gated frames to a prepared branch and immediately executes the corresponding action without generating new text. In paired trials with pre-registered endpoints and exact McNemar tests, AAPT improves the success rate from 0.50 to 0.79 within a contested decision window ($p=1.8\times10^{-3}$), while producing no incorrect actions. Both open-loop and predict-and-replan baselines achieve zero success because they still decode during execution. A preparation-time sweep shows that the gain emerges where the latency-based tree-sizing rule predicts, and ablations reveal three key requirements: fast observer decoding, valid tree planning, and accurate branch routing. A pre-registered oracle probe rejects our initial hypothesis and instead points to branch routing as the causal bottleneck. We further reproduce the effect on an independent general-purpose multimodal model over 126 paired trials ($p=4.9\times10^{-13}$). On an external benchmark, AAPT matches the overall performance of a reactive baseline, although the two methods exhibit complementary strengths. Together, these results suggest that AAPT performs best when candidate actions can be enumerated in advance, whereas reactive execution remains stronger when they cannot.

</details>


### [211] [On-Policy and Off-Policy Learning for Large Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28408)
*Imad Aouali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文研究交互式系统中大规模动作空间下的策略学习问题，涵盖在线学习（上下文多臂老虎机）和离线学习两大范式。在线部分提出meTS和dTS两种结构化贝叶斯方法（混合效应汤普森采样和扩散先验汤普森采样）以共享动作间信息并降低有效动作数。离线部分提出基于潜变量的结构化直接方法sDM，发现优化误差在大动作空间中可主导估计误差，引入凹的可高效优化的策略加权对数似然目标，并开发基于指数平滑和PAC-Bayesian界的可微悲观方法以控制正则化重要性采样估计器的偏差-方差权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模动作空间下的上下文多臂老虎机问题面临探索效率低、数据覆盖稀疏、重要性权重方差高、外推偏差大以及优化景观困难等挑战。现有方法在如此大规模的动作空间中难以有效共享动作间信息，且离线学习中的估计与优化误差平衡问题尚未得到充分解决。

Method: 论文分两部分：在线学习方面，提出meTS（混合效应汤普森采样扩展）和dTS（利用扩散启发的先验建模动作间依赖关系），通过贝叶斯结构共享动作间信息；离线学习方面，提出基于潜变量的结构化直接方法sDM，展示优化误差在大动作空间中的主导性，引入凹的可有效优化的策略加权对数似然目标，并发展基于指数平滑和PAC-Bayesian界的可微悲观方法来平衡正则化重要性采样估计的偏差与方差。

Result: 所提出的方法在遗憾界上依赖有效动作数而非全部动作空间大小，实现了更优的理论保证。sDM方法揭示了优化误差在大动作空间中的重要性，新型凹目标函数可高效优化，悲观方法有效控制了偏差-方差权衡。

Conclusion: 结构化的贝叶斯先验和潜变量建模能够有效应对大规模动作空间的挑战，在线和离线两类方法均提供了理论保证和实用算法，为大规模动作空间的策略学习提供了系统性的解决方案。

Abstract: This thesis studies policy learning in interactive systems where an agent observes a context, selects an action from a very large set, and receives partial feedback. The main framework is contextual bandits, with two paradigms: on-policy learning, where the agent interacts sequentially with the environment and minimizes regret, and off-policy learning, where it learns from logged data collected by a logging policy. In large action spaces, both settings face major challenges: inefficient exploration, sparse data coverage, high-variance importance weights, extrapolation bias, and difficult optimization landscapes. The first part develops structured Bayesian methods for on-policy learning. We introduce meTS, a mixed-effect extension of Thompson sampling, and dTS, which leverages diffusion-inspired priors to model dependencies between actions. These methods share information across actions and yield regret guarantees depending on an effective number of actions. The second part addresses off-policy learning. We propose sDM, a structured direct method based on latent variables, show that optimization error can dominate estimation error in large action spaces, and introduce concave, efficiently optimizable policy-weighted log-likelihood objectives. Finally, we develop differentiable pessimistic methods based on exponential smoothing and PAC-Bayesian bounds to control the bias-variance trade-off of regularized importance-sampling estimators.

</details>


### [212] [QAdapt: A Noise-Adaptive Neural Pre-Decoding Framework for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28422)
*Ran Miao,Rui Luo,Xiaohan Shan,Xiaoming Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QAdapt，一个噪声自适应的神经预解码框架，用于表面码量子纠错。它捕获综合征数据中的局部时空相关性，顺序适应演化中的噪声条件同时缓解灾难性遗忘，并将残余综合征转发给传统全局解码器。在110种合成分布外噪声配置下，QAdapt一致降低了逻辑错误率；在Google Willow基准上无需目标域微调，逻辑错误率最多降低5.79%，后端解码延迟最多降低9.32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算依赖量子纠错抑制物理错误，但性能不仅受物理噪声约束，还受经典解码器处理快速生成综合征数据的延迟限制。硬件噪声强、异构且非平稳，加上模拟到硬件的分布偏移会显著降低固定神经解码器的性能，因此需要能够自适应噪声变化的解码方案。

Method: QAdapt框架包含三个关键组件：(1)捕获综合征数据中局部时空相关性的神经预解码器；(2)顺序适应演化噪声条件同时缓解灾难性遗忘的适应机制；(3)将残余综合征转发给传统全局解码器的接口设计。

Result: 在110种合成的分布外噪声配置的旋转表面码存储电路中，QAdapt相对于神经预解码基线一致降低了逻辑错误率。在Google Willow基准数据上，无需目标域微调，逻辑错误率最多降低5.79%，后端解码延迟最多降低9.32%。

Conclusion: QAdapt提供了一种实用且与解码器兼容的方法，能在演化的硬件噪声条件下提高量子纠错的鲁棒性和后端解码效率。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) relies on quantum error correction to suppress physical errors and preserve logical information at scale. In practice, however, performance is constrained not only by physical noise but also by the latency of classical decoders processing rapidly generated syndrome data. This challenge is exacerbated by hardware noise that is strong, heterogeneous, and nonstationary, as well as by the simulation-to-hardware distribution shift that can substantially degrade fixed neural decoders. We present QAdapt, a noise-adaptive neural pre-decoding framework for surface-code quantum error correction. QAdapt captures local spatiotemporal correlations in syndrome data, sequentially adapts to evolving noise conditions while mitigating catastrophic forgetting, and forwards the residual syndrome to a conventional global decoder. Across 110 synthetic out-of-distribution noise configurations for rotated surface-code memory circuits, QAdapt consistently reduces the logical error rate relative to the neural pre-decoding baseline. On Google's Willow benchmark data, without target-domain fine-tuning, it achieves reductions of up to 5.79 percent in logical error rate and 9.32 percent in backend decoding latency on the residual syndrome. These results demonstrate that QAdapt provides a practical and decoder-compatible approach to improving the robustness and backend decoding efficiency of quantum error correction under evolving hardware noise.

</details>


### [213] [Kohn-Sham Spectral Embedding on Sparse Graphs at the Nishimori Temperature for Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28428)
*V. S. Usatyuk,D. A. Sapozhnikov,S. I. Egorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kohn-Sham谱嵌入（KSSE），一种物理启发的能量基模型，用稀疏图谱嵌入替代密集CNN分类器。通过在准循环LDPC图上构建Kohn-Sham哈密顿量的正则化拉普拉斯算子，利用FFT在O(N log N)时间内求解谱问题，并通过"星域手术"优化图拓扑。在ImageNet-1000上以21.24M参数达到88.93% Top-1准确率，超越Swin-L并匹敌ViT-H/14。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密集CNN分类器参数量大、计算成本高，且缺乏物理可解释性。作者希望利用凝聚态物理中的Kohn-Sham理论、随机键伊辛模型和谱图理论，构建一个参数高效、计算轻量且具有理论保证的分类模型，以更小的模型足迹达到甚至超越大规模Transformer模型的性能。

Method: KSSE将预训练特征映射到准循环LDPC图，构建正则化拉普拉斯算子作为Kohn-Sham哈密顿量。通过FFT（利用Z/pZ的Pontryagin自对偶性）求解D个独立通道谱问题，时间复杂度为O(N log N + k²_mode N)。图拓扑通过"星域手术"优化：构建局部凸性的边移位，限制残余挫败度ρ(Bγ)≤1+δ。多尺度分形分析（D2谱）确认从粗糙区域（D2>3）到星域盆地（D2<1）的景观转变。论文还证明了六个理论结果，包括广义Ihara-Bass恒等式、陷阱集特征值对应、加性通道可分离性等。

Result: 在ImageNet-1000转导协议下，使用冻结的EfficientNet-B4特征（D=1792），KSSE以约21.24M参数达到88.93%的Top-1准确率，超越Swin-L（197M，86.4-87.3%）并与ViT-H/14（632M，88.0-89.5%）相当，分别将模型体积缩小了10倍和30倍。

Conclusion: KSSE展示了物理启发的谱嵌入方法在图像分类中的强大潜力，以极小参数规模匹敌大规模Transformer的性能，同时提供了严格的理论保证。该工作为将凝聚态物理、谱图理论和信息论技术融合到深度学习提供了新的范式，强调了理论驱动的架构设计在模型效率方面的价值。

Abstract: We introduce Kohn--Sham Spectral Embedding (KSSE), a physics-inspired energy-based model replacing dense CNN classifiers with a sparse-graph spectral embedding evaluated at the Nishimori temperature of an associated Random-Bond Ising Model. By mapping pre-trained features onto quasi-cyclic low-density parity-check graphs and constructing a regularized Laplacian acting as a Kohn--Sham Hamiltonian, we solve $D$ independent channel spectral problems in $\mathcal{O}(N\log N + k^2_{\text{mode}} N)$ time via FFT on circulant blocks (leveraging Pontryagin self-duality of $\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z}$) and low-order Rayleigh refinement. Graph topology is optimized using \emph{star-domain surgery}: rather than destroying information-carrying codewords by removing frustrated cycles, we construct edge shifts creating local convexity around codewords while bounding residual frustration to $ρ(B_γ)\leq 1+δ$. Multi-scale fractal analysis ($D_2$ spectrum) and fractal learning-rate landscape certifies a landscape transition from rough regimes ($D_2>3$) to star-domain basins ($D_2<1$), enabling Rayleigh refinement with $k_{\text{mode}}=5$ modes. We prove six theoretical results: a generalized Ihara--Bass identity linking belief propagation to the Laplacian; trapping-set eigenvalue correspondence; additive channel separability with an explicit exchange-correlation bound; a surgery theorem bounding frustration with attractor width $Ω(1/\sqrt{d_{\min}})$; a quasi-stationarity perturbation bound; and a fixed-point convergence theorem. In a transductive protocol on ImageNet-1000 with frozen EfficientNet-B4 features ($D=1792$), KSSE achieves \textbf{88.93\%} Top-1 accuracy using $\approx 21.24$M parameters, outperforming Swin-L (197M, 86.4--87.3\%) and matching ViT-H/14 (632M, 88.0--89.5\%) under standard inductive setups, while reducing model footprint by $10\times$ and $30\times$, respectively.

</details>


### [214] [Oracle-Budgeted Molecular Optimization with Short-Term Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28437)
*Jiannan Yang,Veronika Thost,Xiang Ling,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"短期图记忆"的即插即用模块，用于在有限的oracle预算下进行分子优化。该模块通过在线图神经网络代理对每轮候选分子进行预筛选，使有限的oracle预算优先用于预测效用更高的分子，从而在不增加oracle调用成本的情况下提升优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子优化通常在有限的oracle预算下进行，因此决定评估哪些分子与决定生成哪些分子同样重要。现有的生成器在利用先前评估结果来指导后续查询方面存在不足，需要一种方法在不改变生成器架构和更新规则的前提下，更有效地分配oracle预算。

Method: 引入短期图记忆模块，该模块维护一个在线图神经网络替代模型，对每轮生成的候选分子池进行预筛选打分，从而确保固定的oracle预算被用于评估预测效用更高的分子。该模块保持生成器架构和原生更新规则不变，作为即插即用组件集成到现有生成器中。

Result: 在标准分子优化基准测试中，将该模块应用于基于片段的生成器后，平均top-10得分得到提升，且不增加oracle成本，在任何oracle上均不低于基线。在1000次调用的紧凑预算下，该方法在测试的全部四种生成器上均有收益。进一步分析表明，其收益大小与生成器的搜索广度和对oracle反馈的利用效率相关。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种简单有效的方式来更选择性地花费固定oracle预算，并揭示了哪些类型的生成器能从该策略中获益，为分子优化中有限的评估预算分配提供了实用的指导。

Abstract: Molecular optimization is commonly performed under a limited oracle budget, which makes deciding what to evaluate as important as deciding what to generate. We introduce short-term graph memory, a plug-in module that preserves the generator architecture and native update rule while learning from previously evaluated molecules to prioritize subsequent oracle queries. The module maintains an online graph neural surrogate that pre-screens each round's candidate pool, so the fixed oracle budget is spent on molecules with higher predicted utility. Applied to a fragment-based generator on a standard molecular optimization benchmark, it improves the mean top-10 score at no extra oracle cost and never falls behind the base on any oracle; the gain extends to all four generators we tested at a tight budget of one thousand calls. We then analyze how surrogate-guided selection interacts with the exploration and exploitation behavior of different generators. Its benefit at larger budgets is consistent with two properties of the backbone: how broadly it searches, and how effectively its native search already exploits oracle feedback. We provide a simple way to spend a fixed oracle budget more selectively, and evidence on which generators benefit from it.

</details>


### [215] [Cybersecurity Detection Classification with Reasoning-enabled Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28460)
*Amol Khanna,Manu Nandan,Cristian Viorel Popa,Joan Pujol-Roig,Diana Bolocan,Laura Vasilie,Alexandru Apostu,Chase Helwig,Mihaela Gaman,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff,Chase Midler,Sven Krasser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合链式思维推理的安全运营中心（SOC）告警分流分类器，通过自动化提示优化、自训练和可验证奖励的强化学习进行训练，并引入独立的校准器来修正推理过程导致的标签概率失真。系统在真实Windows终端检测数据上达到82.6%的测试准确率，在高置信度操作点下良性召回率提升43.0%，恶意召回率提升18.3%，优于直接标签LLM分类器和前沿通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SOC面临告警疲劳问题，检测数量超出人员处理能力。现有方法仅提示或微调LLM直接输出分流标签，未训练模型对检测是否为真实威胁进行推理，缺乏可解释性和可靠性。

Method: 采用组合式训练方法：1）自动化提示优化提升提示质量；2）自训练利用伪标签扩充训练数据；3）可验证奖励的强化学习增强推理能力。同时发现CoT推理会降低标签token概率质量，因此训练一个独立校准器，读取完整推理轨迹并估计判定正确的概率。

Result: 系统达到82.6%测试准确率；在高置信度自动分流操作点上，良性召回率提升43.0%，恶意召回率提升18.3%。未训练的置信度判断器会使高置信度召回率降至零，证明校准器训练的必要性。微调的30B模型显著优于前沿通用模型，表明定向训练比单纯扩大模型规模更有效。

Conclusion: 结合CoT推理与独立概率校准的分流系统能有效提升SOC告警分流的准确性和可靠性。定向训练的较小模型可以超越大规模通用模型，为实际安全运营部署提供了高效可行的方案。

Abstract: A major issue in Security Operations Centers (SOCs) is alert fatigue, as the number of detections reported is more than staff can triage in a given day. Prior work prompts or fine-tunes large language models (LLMs) to emit a triage label directly, but does not train them to reason about whether a detection is a genuine threat. We train a chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning-enabled triage classifier on real, human-labeled Windows endpoint detections by combining automated prompt optimization, self-training, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. We find that CoT reasoning also degrades the label-token probabilities that automated triage relies on, so we separately train a calibrator that reads the full reasoning trace and estimates the probability that the verdict is correct. Our system reaches 82.6% test accuracy and, at the high-confidence operating point that governs automated triage, improves benign recall by 43.0% and malicious recall by 18.3% over a direct-label LLM classifier. We further show that the trained calibrator is necessary - an untrained confidence judge collapses high-confidence recall to zero - and that a finetuned 30B model significantly outperforms frontier general-purpose models, motivating targeted training over scale.

</details>


### [216] [Stage-Replay Divergence Follows the KV Cache: Fixed-Prefix Precision Controls and Bidirectional Cache Transplantation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28495)
*Alexander Boesgaard Lorup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对"阶段重放诊断"中"新鲜预填充延续等同于从原始解码器状态延续"这一假设进行了审计。通过在Qwen2.5衍生系统中进行200项匹配实验，发现BF16精度下重现缓存与实时缓存的构造成在分歧，但FP32下完全一致（95% Wilson上界1.88%）。双向移植全部48层K/V缓存可让分歧延续跟随缓存供体（24/24和43/43），证明边界K/V缓存是分歧轨迹的因果充分载体，数值精度调节其行为表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的阶段重放诊断技术依赖于一个关键假设：从新预填充的token前缀继续生成，等价于从最初到达该前缀的解码器状态继续生成。然而这一假设在真实推理场景中从未被严格验证，尤其是在整个推理阶段边界处。作者旨在系统性地审计这一假设的有效性，并探究缓存重建与实时缓存之间可能存在的因果与数值差异。

Method: 设计了一个200项匹配实验，将保留的实时缓存与一次性预填充相同整数token的构造进行对比，并在两侧放置完全相同的副本。采用固定前缀2x2设计，控制所有200个token状态不变，交叉变化构造方式和数值精度（BF16 vs FP32）。此外，提出了一种"前瞻桥接"方法使新增量缓存与实时缓存逐token逐位精确匹配（12/12行）；执行全200项保存账本审计；并在两个不同检查点进行双向移植全部48层K/V层实验（主检查点24/24选定向，后期检查点43/43结果盲复制）。

Result: 在BF16下，副本保持精确，但两种构造在166个后缀和20个正确性标签上存在分歧，准确率差异仅1分（配对95% CI [-3.5, +5.5]）。BF16分歧可重现，而FP32无任何解码分歧（95% Wilson上界1.88%）。前瞻桥接在12/12行上实现逐token逐位精确；全200项账本审计复现所有保留轨迹和比较指纹。双向K/V层移植分别在24/24和43/43个发散延续中使分歧跟随缓存供体。

Conclusion: 精确token重放可以可复现，但无需保持实时状态保真度。在测试状态下，边界K/V缓存是分歧轨迹的因果充分载体，而数值精度是调节其行为表达的关键因素。这表明阶段重放诊断的假设在数值精度充足（FP32）时成立，但在BF16下存在可观察的系统性分歧，而该分歧可通过K/V缓存状态传播来解释。

Abstract: Stage-replay diagnostics reconstruct intermediate token prefixes and treat fresh-prefill continuation as continuation from the decoder state that originally reached the prefix. We audit that assumption at a whole reasoning-stage boundary in a Qwen2.5-derived system. A matched 200-item experiment compares retained live cache with one-shot prefill of identical integer tokens and places an exact replica on both sides. In BF16, replicas remain exact while the constructions differ on 166 suffixes and 20 correctness labels; the accuracy difference is only one point (paired 95% CI [-3.5, +5.5]). A fixed-prefix 2x2 holds all 200 token states constant while crossing construction and precision. The BF16 disagreements recur, whereas FP32 produces no decoded disagreement (95% Wilson upper bound 1.88%). A prospective bridge makes token-by-token incremental and retained live caches bit-exact on 12/12 rows; an all-200 saved-ledger audit reproduces every retained trajectory and comparison fingerprint. Bidirectional transplantation of all 48 key/value layers makes every tested divergent continuation follow its cache donor, both on a selected set at the primary checkpoint (24/24) and an outcome-blind replication at a later checkpoint (43/43). Exact-token replay can therefore be repeatable without preserving live-state fidelity. On the tested states, boundary K/V cache is a causally sufficient carrier of the divergent trajectory, while numerical precision moderates its behavioral expression.

</details>


### [217] [The Role of Causality in Algorithmic Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28497)
*Srikanth Avasarala,Varun Gupta,Shahin Jabbari,Saber Salehkaleybar,Juba Ziani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果表现力框架的算法追索方法，通过结构性因果模型建模追索行动对真实标签和特征的影响，解决传统追索策略因忽略因果结构而导致的预测精度下降和模型重训练失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法追索方法仅关注翻转模型预测结果，未考虑推荐行为是否真正提升个体真实资质或仅导致策略性博弈，导致部署后的追索策略引发行为反应、降低预测精度并在模型重训练后失效。

Method: 提出因果表现力框架（causal performative framework），通过结构性因果模型（SCM）建模追索行动在特征间的传播及其对真实标签的影响；将因果响应转化为非凸优化问题，刻画表现力稳定解存在的条件，并通过简单迭代动力学高效求解。

Result: 在半合成数据和真实信贷数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在各项指标上一致优于标准经验风险最小化（ERM），并能减少因策略性主体行为导致分布偏移而进行的反复模型重训练需求。

Conclusion: 忽略因果结构的追索策略会引发大规模、错位的行为反应，而因果追索能够达到稳定均衡，降低博弈激励，提升追索策略的长期有效性和模型的稳定性。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse aims to provide individuals with actionable changes to improve their predicted outcomes in high-stakes classification settings, such as loan and mortgage applications. However, most existing approaches focus only on flipping a model's prediction, without accounting for whether the recommended changes lead to genuine improvement in an individual's true qualifications or merely enable strategic gaming of the classifier. Consequently, deployed recourse policies can induce behavioral responses that degrade predictive accuracy and become ineffective after model retraining.
  In this work, we formalize this failure mode through a causal performative framework for recourse. We model how recourse actions propagate through a structural causal model, capturing interactions among features as well as their effect on the true label. These causal responses induce a non-convex optimization problem, even under standard convex losses. We characterize conditions under which performatively stable solutions exist and can be efficiently computed via simple iterative dynamics. Our analysis reveals that recourse policies that ignore causal structure can induce large, misaligned behavioral responses, whereas causal recourse leads to stable equilibria that reduce incentives for gaming. Experiments on both semi-synthetic and real credit datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms standard empirical risk minimization while reducing the need for repeated model retraining to accommodate distribution shifts caused by strategic agent behavior.

</details>


### [218] [Same Graph Cross-Task Transfer in GNNs: Protocols and Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28525)
*Neelam Akula,Surbhi Kumar,Murat Kantarcioglu,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了同一图上节点分类（NC）与链接预测（LP）之间的跨任务迁移问题，提出了一种无泄漏的评估协议，发现NC→LP在同质图上持续有益，而LP→NC则脆弱甚至可能损害性能，并提出了CoTask Score（CTS）来量化双任务共享编码器的联合效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于同一图上NC和LP跨任务迁移的评估缺乏统一标准，存在不兼容的数据划分、观测图假设和负采样规则等问题，导致关于跨任务迁移的结论不可靠。

Method: 形式化定义了同图NC-LP迁移问题，提出无泄漏评估协议（固定节点和边划分、共享消息传递图排除被评估边、LP使用固定负样本），在三种骨干网络（GCN、GraphSAGE、GPS）上进行系统实验，并引入CoTask Score（CTS）指标来评估共享编码器同时服务两个任务的联合效用。

Result: 发现迁移具有强烈的方向性：NC→LP在同质图上始终有益；LP→NC在朴素表示复用下很脆弱甚至降低准确率，仅在结构主导（LP容易但NC未饱和）的情境下可靠为正。简单的数据集统计特征（尤其是同质性）可以指导机制选择并避免负迁移。

Conclusion: 同图NC-LP迁移的方向性可预测，同质性等简单统计量可用于指导迁移机制选择。LP可被视为一种结构预训练，但需要谨慎设计才能避免负迁移。

Abstract: Many real-world graphs support multiple predictive tasks over the same underlying structure, creating an opportunity to reuse supervision across node classification (NC) and link prediction (LP). However, existing evaluations often rely on incompatible splits, observed-graph assumptions, and negative sampling rules, making conclusions about same-graph cross-task transfer unreliable. We formalize same-graph NC-LP transfer and propose a leakage-free protocol that fixes node and edge splits, uses a shared message-passing graph that excludes evaluated edges, and employs fixed negatives for LP. Across three backbones (GCN, GraphSAGE, GPS), we find that transfer is strongly directional and predictable: NC $\to$ LP is consistently beneficial on homophilic graphs, while LP $\to$ NC is fragile and can even degrade accuracy under naive representation reuse. LP $\to$ NC becomes reliably positive mainly in a structure-dominant regime where LP is easy but NC is unsaturated, suggesting that LP acts as structural pretraining. Finally, we introduce the CoTask Score (CTS) to summarize joint NC+LP utility when a shared encoder must serve both tasks, and show that simple dataset statistics, especially homophily, can guide mechanism choice and help avoid negative transfer.

</details>


### [219] [APO: Unsupervised Atomic Policy Optimization for 3D Structure Prediction of Atomic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28553)
*Shentong Mo,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出APO（Atomic Policy Optimization），一种完全无监督的流匹配模型对齐框架，通过群相对策略优化和双奖励机制（潜结构模式强化+热力学稳定性约束），无需地面真值坐标即可实现对3D原子结构的预测对齐，在晶体和抗体结构预测任务上超越全监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配模型（如FlowDPO）依赖监督偏好学习对齐地面真值坐标，但获取新晶体相或全新蛋白质的实验标签成本极高，形成数据稀缺场景下的瓶颈。需要一种无需参考结构的无监督对齐方法。

Method: 提出APO框架，核心包含：将群相对策略优化（GRPO）适配到3D原子环境；设计双奖励机制——(i) 通过样本相似性的特征分解强化策略的主导潜结构模式，(ii) 强制热力学稳定性；使模型在采样群体内自我识别物理上合理的构象。

Result: 在晶体和抗体结构预测基准上，APO一致超越全监督基线，在匹配率和结构保真度上达到新SOTA；同时有效"拉直"概率路径，显著提升推理效率。

Conclusion: 内在物理一致性可以作为比含噪声的监督坐标匹配更优越的对齐指导信号，为数据稀缺场景下的3D结构建模提供了可行的无监督对齐范式。

Abstract: Predicting the 3D structures of atomic systems is fundamental to advancing material science and drug discovery. While flow-matching models (, FlowDPO) have recently shown promise in this domain, their performance relies heavily on alignment with ground-truth coordinates via supervised preference learning. However, obtaining experimental labels for novel crystal phases or de novo proteins is prohibitively expensive, creating a bottleneck for structural modeling in data-scarce regimes. In this work, we propose (Atomic Policy Optimization), a fully unsupervised alignment framework that eliminates the need for ground-truth reference structures. APO adapts group-relative policy optimization to 3D atomic environments, utilizing a novel dual-reward mechanism: (i) a that reinforces the policy's dominant latent structural modes through eigen-decomposition of sample similarities, and (ii) a that enforces thermodynamic stability. Our framework enables the model to ``self-correct'' by identifying physically plausible configurations within sampled groups. Extensive benchmarks on crystal and antibody structure prediction demonstrate that APO consistently outperforms fully supervised baselines, achieving a new state-of-the-art in match rates and structural fidelity. Furthermore, we show that APO effectively straightens probability paths, significantly improving inference efficiency. Our results suggest that intrinsic physical consistency can serve as a superior guide for alignment compared to noisy, supervised coordinate matching.

</details>


### [220] [$β$-OPSD: Deriving with Policy Optimization, Training with Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28582)
*Jiawei Xu,Minghui Liu,Juzheng Zhang,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出β-OPSD，将vanilla OPSD（β=1的KL惩罚策略优化特例）推广为可控正则化参数，通过几何插值参考策略与特权教师得到最优策略，并利用token级logits混合的蒸馏目标近似实现，显著提升推理模型的优化稳定性和推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vanilla OPSD在实践中表现脆弱、需要大量工程调优，其根本原因是它固定了KL惩罚权重β=1，缺乏可控的正则化能力。作者希望将其推广为可控参数，在参考策略约束与特权教师引导之间灵活权衡，从而提升稳定性和性能。

Method: 作者将vanilla OPSD重新定义为β=1的特例，推广为一般化的β-OPSD目标。推导出该目标的闭式最优策略为参考策略与特权教师之间的几何插值，并利用混合token级logits实现这一插值作为蒸馏目标，以低成本蒸馏近似昂贵的策略优化。同时引入return-to-go信用分配来对齐token更新与序列级目标。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，β-OPSD一致优于vanilla OPSD，在优化稳定性和下游推理性能上均有提升。

Conclusion: 该工作为从自我蒸馏到策略优化之间建立了理论桥梁，在不牺牲OPSD实用效率的前提下提供了更稳健、可调的正则化途径，为推理语言模型的训练提供了更可靠的方法。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is a promising approach to improve reasoning language models, but it remains brittle in practice: making it work reliably often requires substantial engineering effort. We identify a structural source of this difficulty: vanilla OPSD is precisely the $β=1$ member of a broader policy-optimization family, where $β$ weights the KL penalty anchoring the student to a reference policy. This equivalence turns $β$ from an implicit value fixed at one into a controllable regularization parameter, yielding a more general formulation that trades off proximity to a reference policy against privileged teacher guidance. We introduce $β$-OPSD and derive its optimal policy as a geometric interpolation between the reference policy and the privileged teacher. Directly optimizing this objective with reinforcement learning, however, would be costly and high-variance. Rather than optimize the RL objective directly, we turn its closed-form solution into a distillation target. Each value of $β$ selects a target along the reference-to-teacher path, which we implement efficiently by mixing their token-level logits. In this way, inexpensive distillation approximates the solution of expensive policy optimization. Return-to-go credit assignment further aligns token updates with the sequence-level objective while retaining the simplicity of OPSD. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that $β$-OPSD consistently outperforms vanilla OPSD, improving optimization stability and downstream reasoning performance. Our results provide a principled route from self-distillation to policy optimization and back without sacrificing the efficiency that makes OPSD practical.

</details>


### [221] [KAISEN: Reproducible Subgroup Fairness Auditing for Clinical Risk Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28608)
*Sparsh Roy,Samuel Girmachew,Nishita Chavan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KAISEN五阶段审计流水线（亚组分层、差异度量、机制诊断、事后缓解、漂移监测），在16个疾病任务、15个社会决定因素轴和3个预设交叉组的合成基准上评估其失效边界。主要发现：显著性统计需按最小可检测效应标准化；阈值优化比Platt缩放更有效降低EOD；机制诊断在代理变量设定错误时完全失效且无失败信号；CUSUM阈值在不同队列间不可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床风险模型在总体表现良好时，可能对特定患者亚组产生不同错误率。现有审计流水线各组件很少被压力测试，导致不清楚哪些部分在什么条件下可被信任。

Method: 构建合成基准（16个疾病任务、15个社会决定因素轴、3个预设交叉组）评估KAISEN五阶段审计流水线，通过已知真值检验各组件的失效边界，包括显著性统计标准化、事后缓解方法对比、机制诊断鲁棒性以及CUSUM阈值迁移性。

Result: (i) 显著性计数与原始EOD的秩相关为0.56，经最小可检测效应标准化后升至0.78；(ii) 阈值优化在48/48次运行中降低EOD（平均差-0.285），而Platt缩放在19/48次中改善且均值近零；(iii) 机制诊断在144/144个受控案例中正确，但在代理变量设定错误时48个模型驱动案例全部失败且无失败信号；(iv) CUSUM假警报和漏检更多由队列种子而非疾病决定，阈值不可跨队列迁移。

Conclusion: 审计流水线各组件有其各自的失效条件：显著性需考虑最小可检测效应；审计应报告缓解效果的方差而非均值；机制诊断在代理设定错误时不可信且无法自检；漂移检测阈值需按队列校准。结果均为合成数据，不建立临床有效性。

Abstract: Clinical risk models routinely achieve strong aggregate performance while producing materially different error rates across patient subgroups. Audit pipelines have been proposed to catch this, but their components are rarely stress-tested, so it is unclear which parts of an audit can be trusted and under what conditions. We present KAISEN, a five-phase audit pipeline covering subgroup stratification, disparity measurement, mechanism diagnostics, post-hoc mitigation, and drift monitoring, evaluated to the point of failure on a synthetic benchmark of 16 disease tasks, 15 social-determinant axes from Healthy People 2030, and three prespecified intersections. Four findings follow. (i) Significance tracks each axis's gap against its own minimum detectable effect: rank correlation between significance count and raw equalized-odds difference (EOD) across the 15 axes is rho = 0.56, rising to rho = 0.78 once EOD is standardized by that floor. (ii) Per-group threshold optimization reduces EOD in 48 of 48 held-out runs (paired delta = -0.285, 95% CI [-0.313, -0.252]), while group-wise Platt scaling -- the better calibrator -- behaves as a coin flip on EOD (19 of 48 runs improved, 95% CI [0.26, 0.55]) with mean effect near zero, so what an audit should report is the variance, not the average. (iii) The mechanism diagnostic classifies 144 of 144 controlled cases correctly but recovers none of 48 model-driven cases under proxy misspecification, with no signal that it failed. (iv) CUSUM failures and false alarms track cohort realization far more than disease: at the reference threshold, all 27 false alarms and 7 of 8 missed shifts come from different seeds (chi-squared p = 0.002), so a threshold tuned on one cohort fails to transfer. All results are synthetic with known ground truth and do not establish clinical validity. Code, artifacts, and scripts reproducing every number are released.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [Multi-Head Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27230)
*Cheng Luo,Zefan Cai,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多头注意力残差（MHAR）机制，将注意力残差中的单一查询重塑为多个子空间头，每个头独立对深度历史做softmax，使不同特征子空间可以按各自偏好读取历史层信息。MHAR在100M至1B规模上均优于标准Transformer及现有方法，且验证损失对头数呈U型分布，最优为H=4或H=8。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer通过单一加性残差流传播信息，每层只读取最新状态；注意力残差虽允许每子层关注深度历史，但使用单一查询共享全部宽度，导致不同特征子空间被迫使用同一分布读取历史。子空间对读取层的分歧随模型宽度增大而加剧，这种妥协的代价也随之增长。

Method: 将注意力残差的路由查询重塑为H个逐子空间头，每个头拥有自己对深度历史的softmax分布，读取变为块对角结构。重塑不增加任何参数，计算开销可忽略，H=1时恰好还原为原始注意力残差。同时设计了融合Triton路由核以提升训练吞吐量，并采用Delta注意力残差的恒等保持转换支持8B规模的中期训练。

Result: 在去重、质量过滤且偏重STEM与代码的Nemotron退火语料上从头训练，MHAR在100M、350M、1B规模上分别将验证损失降低-0.061、-0.149和-0.140；在所有设置上击败其余四种方法，且增益随规模增大而增强。头数是真实设计维度：验证损失对H呈U型，最优为H=4或H=8，过大分割（H=16）会损失部分增益。查询探针实验证实子空间分歧是驱动因素。训练吞吐量提升至基线0.55-0.88倍，峰值内存接近基线。8B中期训练在GSM8K和GPQA上分别提升+3.2和+3.1。

Conclusion: MHAR通过为不同特征子空间分配独立的历史路由分布，有效解决了注意力残差中单一查询带来的子空间冲突问题。验证损失随规模增大增益更强，且头数存在清晰的最优范围（H=4~8），表明这是一个需要设计的维度而非任意自由度。该机制在训练效率和下游任务表现上均具备实用性，为大规模Transformer架构改进提供了新方向。

Abstract: Transformers propagate information across depth through a single additive residual stream: every sublayer reads only the most recent state. Attention residuals relax this by letting each sublayer attend, through a learned softmax. However, that read uses a single query shared across the entire width, so every feature subspace must read the depth history through one distribution. The cost of this forced compromise grows with how much the subspaces disagree about which layers to read, and disagreement grows with model width. We introduce Multi-Head Attention Residuals (MHAR): the routing query is reshaped into H per-subspace heads, each with its own softmax over the depth history. The read becomes block-diagonal, the reshape adds zero parameters and negligible compute, and H = 1 recovers attention residuals exactly. Trained from scratch on a deduplicated Nemotron-based anneal corpus that is quality-filtered and STEM- and code-heavy, MHAR improves validation loss over a standard Transformer at 100M, 350M, and 1B (-0.061, -0.149, and -0.140). It achieves the best result among four methods in every setting, with the gain increasing from 100M to the larger scales. The head count is a real design axis rather than a free knob: validation loss is U-shaped with respect to H, with a flat optimum at H = 4 or H = 8 across scales. We adopt H = 8 for large-scale models; over-splitting beyond this point (H = 16) consistently gives back part of the gain. A direct probe of the trained queries confirms that learned subspace disagreement is the underlying driver. Fused Triton routing kernels increase attention-residual training throughput from 0.2-0.5x to 0.55-0.88x of the baseline while maintaining near-baseline peak memory. An identity-preserving conversion using delta attention residuals supports 8B mid-training, yielding improvements of +3.2 on GSM8K and +3.1 on GPQA.

</details>


### [223] [KernelGenBench: A Multi-Source and Multi-Chip Benchmark for LLM-based Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27231)
*Peiyu Zang,Jian Tao,Jialing Zhang,Yichen Yuan,Wentao Zhang,Guang Liu,Yonghua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了KernelGenBench，一个统一的基准测试框架，用于系统评估LLM和智能体生成的Triton内核，涵盖多样化的算子来源和异构硬件平台。包含两个子基准：KernelGenBench-MS（210个算子，三个来源）和KernelGenBench-MC（110个算子，六个硬件平台）。大规模评估（消耗超过150亿token）揭示了智能体方法优于纯LLM采样、跨平台性能显著退化以及内核生成成本高昂等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的兴起，自动生成高效加速器内核成为可能，但目前缺乏一个全面的基准来严格评估LLM生成的内核在多样化算子来源和异构硬件平台上的表现。现有评估多局限于PyTorch中心化任务，无法全面反映实际需求。

Method: 构建了KernelGenBench统一基准框架，包含两个子基准：KernelGenBench-MS评估来自三个来源的210个算子（超越标准PyTorch中心化任务），KernelGenBench-MC使用110个算子子集衡量在六个异构硬件平台上的性能可移植性。通过消耗超过150亿token的大规模评估来验证各种LLM和智能体方法。

Result: 实验发现：(1)智能体方法持续优于纯LLM采样方法，其中cuBLAS算子对所有方法最具挑战性；(2)不同硬件平台间生成性能差异显著，即使最新的内核专用智能体也面临严重的跨平台性能退化（如AutoKernel从NVIDIA上的87%跌至平台E上的25%）；(3)自主内核生成成本高昂，专用智能体方法平均每个成功算子消耗511万token（AKO4all达到519万），比简单LLM采样方法高出数个数量级。

Conclusion: 本文填补了LLM生成内核缺乏统一评估基准的空白，系统揭示了当前智能体方法在算子来源覆盖、跨硬件平台性能和生成成本方面的关键瓶颈，为未来内核生成研究提供了重要的评测基准和方向指引。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have significantly increased the demand for efficient accelerator kernels, but kernel development remains a highly specialized and labor-intensive task. The recent rise of LLMs and agentic frameworks offers a promising pathway toward automatic kernel generation. However, despite rapid progress, there is still no comprehensive benchmark to rigorously evaluate LLM-generated kernels across diverse operator sources or heterogeneous hardware platforms. We present KernelGenBench, a unified benchmark for systematically evaluating LLM- and agent-generated Triton kernels across diverse operator sources and heterogeneous hardware platforms. It comprises two complementary sub-benchmarks: KernelGenBench-MS (Multi-Source), evaluating 210 operators from three sources beyond standard PyTorch-centric tasks, and KernelGenBench-MC (Multi-Chip), measuring performance portability across six heterogeneous hardware platforms using a 110-operator subset. Our large-scale evaluation, consuming over 15 billion tokens, shows: (1) agent-based methods consistently outperform pure LLM sampling methods, while cuBLAS operators are the most challenging across all methods; (2) generation performance varies significantly across hardware platforms, with even recent kernel-specialized agents experiencing severe cross-platform degradation (e.g., AutoKernel drops from 87% on NVIDIA to 25% on Platform E); (3) autonomous kernel generation remains highly cost-intensive, with specialized agent methods averaging 5.11 million tokens per successful operator (AKO4all reaches 5.19 million), orders of magnitude higher than simple LLM sampling approaches.

</details>


### [224] [RadHarmony: Radiological Data Handling in the Era of Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27235)
*Frank Li,Bardia Khosravi,Mohammadreza Chavoshi,Theo Dapamede,YoungSeok Jeon,Janice Newsome,Hari Trivedi,Judy Gichoya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RadHarmony是一个开源的Python库，提供统一API用于加载、整合和增强放射学影像数据集，主要支持胸部X光片，并初步支持CT和MRI。它标准化了24个公共数据集的元数据，支持分类标签、分割掩码、边界框和放射报告文本，并引入AI-agent技能辅助新数据集的集成流程。通过预训练RadHarmony-ViT视觉Transformer基线验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在放射学影像上训练深度学习模型需要整合来自不同来源、文件格式、目录布局、标签模式和注释类型的异构数据集，这一过程复杂且耗时。现有工具缺乏统一的数据标准化和加载方案。

Method: 构建RadHarmony库，包含：(1) 将24个公共数据集的元数据标准化为统一表格格式；(2) 封装MONAI的map-style数据集，实现深度学习就绪的样本交付和可选的磁盘缓存；(3) 通过统一接口支持分类标签、分割掩码、边界框和放射报告文本；(4) 提供交互式可视化工具用于数据集探索和验证；(5) 引入AI-agent技能引导从原始数据检查到代码生成和测试的完整集成工作流。

Result: 通过预训练RadHarmony-ViT（参考视觉Transformer基线），结合三个异构胸部X光数据集且无需任何数据集特定代码，验证了库的实用性和有效性。

Conclusion: RadHarmony有效降低了放射学影像数据集整合的壁垒，为多源异质数据集提供了统一的深度学习训练解决方案，代码和预训练模型权重已开源。

Abstract: Training deep learning models on radiological images requires integrating heterogeneous datasets across different sources, file formats, directory layouts, label schemas, and annotation types. We present RadHarmony, an open-source Python library that provides a unified API for loading, harmonizing, and augmenting radiological datasets, with a primary focus on chest radiographs and early support for computed tomography (CT) and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). RadHarmony standardizes metadata from 24 public datasets into a single tabular format, wraps MONAI's map-style datasets for deep-learning-ready sample delivery with optional on-disk caching, and supports classification labels, segmentation masks, bounding boxes, and radiology report text through a single interface, with an interactive visualization tool for dataset exploration and verification. To lower the barrier for integrating new datasets, RadHarmony introduces an AI-agent skill that guides the full integration workflow from raw data inspection through code generation and testing. We demonstrate the library's utility by pretraining RadHarmony-ViT, a reference vision transformer baseline that combines three heterogeneous chest radiograph datasets with no dataset-specific code. The code and pretrained model weights are available at https://github.com/f10409/RadHarmony.

</details>


### [225] [Asymmetric Collapse in Model Merging: When Refusal Over- writes Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27240)
*Aarnav Choudhary,Matheus Fonseca Rocha,Jiwon Seo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过Gemma-3-1B-IT模型的两个安全相关微调（CARES危害分类和WildJailbreak对抗拒绝）的受控案例研究，发现标准模型合并方法（Linear、SLERP、TIES、DARE-TIES）在保留多个安全行为时存在显著不对称性：攻击抵抗能力（81-85%）远超分类准确率（至多12.9%）。原因不是任务向量方向冲突（余弦相似度仅0.011），而是拒绝微调产生更大的逐层任务向量幅值，导致对幅值敏感的合并方法偏向拒绝更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并常被用于无需额外训练即可结合多个微调模型的能力，但目前尚不清楚标准合并方法是否能同时保留多个安全相关行为，这一问题对安全关键应用至关重要。

Method: 使用两个Gemma-3-1B-IT微调模型分别针对CARES危害级别分类和WildJailbreak对抗拒绝两个互补安全目标，采用Linear、SLERP、TIES和DARE-TIES四种合并方法，评估合并模型在分类准确率、攻击抵抗和良性合规方面的表现，并进行权重空间分析（任务向量方向与幅值）。

Result: 四种合并方法均显示攻击抵抗能力显著优于分类准确率：拒绝率保持在81-85%，而CARES分类准确率最高仅12.9%。任务向量接近正交（余弦相似度0.011），排除方向冲突因素；但拒绝微调的逐层任务向量幅值更大，导致幅值敏感的合并方法偏向保留拒绝行为。

Conclusion: 标准模型合并方法在安全任务向量尺度差异显著时，会将安全识别能力退化为广泛的拒绝行为，表明现有合并方法在多安全行为保持方面存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Model merging is often used to combine capabilities from separately fine-tuned models without additional training, but it is unclear whether standard merging methods preserve multiple safety-relevant behaviors simultaneously. We study this question through a controlled case study using two Gemma-3-1B-IT finetunes on two complementary safety objectives: CARES harm-level classification and WildJailbreak adversarial refusal. We merge the two fine-tunes using Linear, SLERP, TIES, and DARE-TIES, and evaluate the merged models on classification accuracy, attack resistance, and benign compliance. Across all four methods, attack resistance transfers significantly more than classification accuracy: merged models retain 81-85% jailbreak refusal rates while CARES accuracy falls to at most 12.9%. Weight-space measurements suggest that this asymmetry is not caused by strongly opposing task-vector directions: the two task vectors are nearly orthogonal (cosine similarity 0.011). Instead, the refusal fine-tune induces consistently larger per-layer task-vector magnitudes, causing magnitude-sensitive methods to favor refusal updates. These results show that standard model merging can collapse safety recognition into broad refusal when safety-relevant task vectors differ substantially in scale.

</details>


### [226] [Divergence Decoding: Training-Free Capability Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27248)
*Yimi Wang,Hao Li,Shuo Yang,He Cao,Dechen Zhang,Ziang Wu,Zhiyuan Yan,Fanyang Mo,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Divergence Decoding，一种无需训练的推理时框架，通过Jensen-Shannon散度监测通用大模型与领域专精模型在逐token层面的分布分歧，动态路由控制权以融合两者能力，在GPQA、ChemBench和ChemCoTBench等科学基准上超越单一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型面临通用性与专精性的两难困境：通用模型推理能力强但缺乏专业知识，而领域专精模型虽然知识丰富却存在逻辑能力下降和鲁棒性降低等副作用。

Method: 将投机解码的"草稿-验证"机制重构为自适应路由框架，核心是利用Jensen-Shannon散度在每一步token生成时监控通用模型与专精模型之间的分布分歧。当专精模型产生显著分歧时，识别为潜在推理风险并即时将控制权切换给通用模型，实现推理时的动态能力融合。

Result: 在Qwen和Llama多种模型系列上，针对GPQA、ChemBench和ChemCoTBench等科学基准的评估表明，Divergence Decoding同时优于领域专精模型和通用模型，超越了大多数单模型基线的性能。

Conclusion: Divergence Decoding提供了一种通用且无需训练的范式，通过适应性的推理时协作实现不同LLM能力的高效融合，为模型能力组合提供了新思路。

Abstract: While large language models excel in reasoning, these generalists often lack knowledge for specialized scientific domains. Conversely, domain models~(specialists), while knowledgeable, suffer from specialization side-effects including diminished logic and reduced robustness.To address this dilemma, we introduce Divergence Decoding, a training-free framework for capability fusion. It reconstructs the "draft-and-verify" skeleton of speculative decoding into an adaptive routing mechanism. The core is using Jensen-Shannon divergence to monitor the distributional disagreement between the two models at each token. When the specialist exhibits significant divergence, our method identifies it as a potential reasoning risk and instantaneously routes control to the generalist. This allows the dynamic injection of general reasoning while preserving domain expertise, achieving inference-time policy composition of the generalist and the specialist.We evaluate Divergence Decoding across diverse model families (Qwen and Llama series) on challenging scientific benchmarks (GPQA, ChemBench, and ChemCoTBench). Experimental results demonstrate that Divergence Decoding outperforms both the domain-specialized and general-purpose models, effectively surpassing the performance of most single-model baseline. This suggests that Divergence Decoding provides a general, training-free paradigm for fusing diverse LLM capabilities through adaptive inference-time collaboration.

</details>


### [227] [PIE-APT: A Unified Framework for Temporal Planning and Contradiction Hunting via Incremental Direct-Derivation Abduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27287)
*Amir Hossein Sharafi,Alireza Shahbazi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个在动态知识图谱（DKG）上进行推理与规划的统一框架，包含两个集成模块：PIE-Abducer（增量直接推导溯因）和 PIE-APT（时序知识图谱的溯因规划），基于高表达力的描述逻辑原生操作，通过非单调更新和直接反驳推断来避免最小碰集枚举，保持逻辑可判定性，并在四个OWL基准上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态知识图谱上的推理与规划在开放世界环境中面临不完整信息的重大挑战。现有动作形式化方法常遭遇可判定性问题与分支问题，而通过结构溯因管理不完整知识需要大规模的组合搜索，效率低下。

Method: 构建统一框架，包含两个集成模块。PIE-Abducer通过将目标目标的逻辑否定注入一致分支，直接从反驳后果中提取缺失前提，规避传统最小碰集（MHS）枚举。PIE-APT采用递归的生成-测试架构，将反向链式A*搜索与PIE-Abducer交错进行，达到有界的因果深度，再通过前向链时序投影严格验证。状态沿线性时间线建模为对演绎封闭DL理论的非单调更新，将增量推理器视为黑盒，动作原生用OWL表示，无需外部模态算子，保持逻辑可判定性。

Result: 在四个OWL基准上评估，涵盖参数化目标与见证搜索、搜索中DL蕴含、开放世界假设注入和对抗性矛盾查找等语义能力。结果表明该方法相对经典规划器具有定性优势，且直接推导方法在溯因丰富过程中定量优于保持MHS忠实性的基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文证明基于描述逻辑原生的溯因规划框架能够在保持逻辑可判定性的同时，有效处理开放世界中动态知识图谱的不完整信息，且直接推导溯因方法在性能上优于传统的最小碰集枚举方法。

Abstract: Reasoning and planning over Dynamic Knowledge Graphs (DKGs) present significant challenges, especially in open-world environments with incomplete information. Existing action formalisms often face decidability issues and the Ramification Problem, while managing incomplete knowledge via structural abduction requires expansive combinatorial search. This paper introduces a unified framework with two integrated modules---\textbf{PIE-Abducer} (incremental direct-derivation abduction) and \textbf{PIE-APT} (Abductive Planning for Temporal KGs)---operating natively on the highly expressive Description Logic. We model state transitions along a linear timeline as non-monotonic updates to deductively closed DL theories. Treating the incremental reasoner as a black-box and representing actions natively in OWL without external modal operators preserves logical decidability. To address incomplete knowledge, \textbf{PIE-Abducer} circumvents traditional Minimal Hitting Set (MHS) enumeration. Instead of combinatorial syntactic search, it injects the logical negation of a target goal into a consistent branch and extracts missing premises via direct refutation consequences. \textbf{PIE-APT} then employs a recursive \textit{Generate-and-Test} architecture, interleaving backward-chaining A* search with \textbf{PIE-Abducer} up to a bounded causal depth, followed by strict validation via forward-chaining Temporal Projection. We evaluate four OWL benchmarks stressing semantic abilities absent in classical planning: parameterized goals with witness search, mid-search DL entailment, open-world assumption injection, and adversarial contradiction hunting. Results demonstrate qualitative superiority over classical planners and prove our direct-derivation approach quantitatively outperforms an MHS-faithful baseline during abductive enrichment.

</details>


### [228] [PAUSE: A User-Centric Benchmark for Personal AI Assistants in Unified Service Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27354)
*Haoyu Chen,Xirui Shi,Yuyao Wang,Jerry Chen,Di Niu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PAUSE，一个面向个人AI助手在状态化、服务集成环境中评估的用户中心基准测试平台，涵盖跨异构资源的任务协调、授权约束和用户模拟评估。实验表明即便最先进的专有模型在需要状态推理和配置感知的场景下任务完成率也不足70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试要么将服务上下文碎片化，要么抽象掉用户状态，无法真实评估个人AI助手在现实服务场景中的用户中心行为表现，需要一个能够捕捉真实部署挑战的评估基准。

Method: PAUSE基准包含多机制评估框架：开放式服务管理任务使用语义和轨迹级行为指标评估，约束密集型任务采用确定性状态验证；通过真实用户模拟实现显式用户-智能体交互；并提出用户中心合成管线以支持可扩展的环境和任务生成。

Result: 最先进的专有模型在需要状态化推理和配置感知的场景下任务完成率低于70%，且存在一致且可解释的失败模式。

Conclusion: PAUSE能够有效暴露当前AI助手在真实个性化服务环境中的能力差距，其用户中心合成管线为基准的可扩展性和未来研究提供了支撑。

Abstract: Personal AI assistants are increasingly deployed as task-oriented, tool-augmented agents that operate within unified service environments to support everyday user activities. In realistic settings, such assistants must reason over persistent user state, respect user-specific configurations and permissions, and sustain long-horizon, constraint-aware interactions across multiple services. Existing benchmarks, however, often fragment service contexts or abstract away user state, limiting their ability to evaluate user-centric personal assistant behavior in realistic service settings. We introduce PAUSE, a user-centric benchmark for evaluating personal AI assistants in stateful, service-integrated environments. PAUSE captures core challenges of real-world assistant deployment by requiring agents to coordinate actions across heterogeneous user-owned resources while maintaining consistency with environment state, authorization constraints over multi-turn interactions. The benchmark incorporates explicit user-agent interaction via realistic user simulation, enabling evaluation beyond static tool execution. To support principled and reproducible evaluation, PAUSE adopts a multi-regime evaluation framework aligned with task characteristics. Open-ended service management tasks are assessed using semantic and trajectory-level behavioral metrics, while constraint-intensive tasks admit deterministic, state-based verification. Benchmark results show that even state-of-the-art proprietary models fail to reach 70% task completion on scenarios requiring stateful reasoning and configuration awareness, revealing consistent and interpretable failure patterns. Finally, we present a user-centric synthesis pipeline that enables scalable generation of coherent service environments, user configurations, and reliably annotated tasks, supporting benchmark extensibility and future research.

</details>


### [229] [SkillMentor: LLM Agent Self-Evolution via Learning Blind-Spot Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27360)
*Xiaoyi Bao,Yuanzhen Xie,Yunzhi Tan,Jinghang Gu,Zhongqing Wang,Chu-Ren Huang,Bo Hu,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillMentor框架，将"盲点诊断"（发现智能体自身未知之处）作为独立于执行的可学习能力，通过强化学习训练Mentor策略来生成诊断任务、识别重复失败模式并策划可复用的纠正技能，在不更新执行器权重或依赖人工标注的情况下，使执行器性能在AppWorld和BFCLv3上平均提升44.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体自我进化研究主要关注"如何行动"的学习，而忽视了"发现自身不知道什么"这一同等重要的能力。已有方法通常假设失败发现是给定的，只关注识别出失败后的修复，而盲点诊断本身是否可以学习尚未被研究。

Method: 提出SkillMentor框架：通过强化学习训练一个Mentor策略，使其能够生成诊断任务、识别重复出现的失败模式，并将这些失败模式策划为可复用的纠正技能。研究设计排除了执行器适应和人类监督两个替代进步来源，确保所有性能提升均源于学到的诊断能力。

Result: 在AppWorld和BFCLv3两个基准上，SkillMentor使执行器性能平均提升44.2%。实验结果表明盲点诊断是可学习的，无需更新执行器权重或依赖人工策划的数据即可实现自我进化。

Conclusion: 盲点诊断本身是一种可学习的能力，它可以作为独立于执行的智能体能力被训练，这为智能体自我进化开辟了新的方向——不需要修改执行器或依赖人类标注，仅通过提升诊断能力就能显著改善整体性能。

Abstract: Agent self-evolution has primarily focused on learning how to act, while overlooking an equally important capability: learning to discover what an agent does not know. Existing approaches typically assume that failure discovery is given, focusing on how to repair failures once they are identified. We ask whether blind-spot diagnosis itself can be learned. We thus study diagnosis as an agent capability separate from execution, and exclude two alternative sources of progress: executor adaptation and human supervision. Under these constraints, performance cannot improve through executor updates or annotated examples, forcing all improvements to originate from the learned diagnostic capability. We propose SkillMentor, which trains a Mentor policy via reinforcement learning to generate diagnostic tasks, identify recurrent failure modes, and curate them into reusable corrective skills. Across AppWorld and BFCLv3, SkillMentor improves executor performance by an average of 44.2%. These results suggest that blind-spot diagnosis is a learnable capability, enabling self-evolution without updating executor weights or relying on human-curated data.

</details>


### [230] [Beyond the Bidirectional Promise: Re-evaluating the Robustness of Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27386)
*Saurabh Yadav,Badri Narayana Patro,Vijay Srinivas Agneeswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了扩散语言模型（DLMs）在自然噪声和对抗攻击下的鲁棒性与校准性能，与自回归（AR）基线进行了对比，发现DLMs虽天然抵御梯度对抗后缀，但面对自然噪声并不具备固有优势，且存在系统性过度自信问题，根源在于解码器路由失败而非输入编码问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型作为自回归生成的替代方案具有双向上下文和迭代细化的优势，但其在自然输入噪声和对抗攻击下的可靠性尚未被充分探索，这一空白需要系统性的实证研究来填补。

Method: 采用两对参数匹配的模型（LLaDA-8B vs LLaMA-3-8B 和 Dream-7B vs Qwen2.5-7B），通过32种自然扰动条件、对抗梯度探测和机制性隐状态分析，进行配对实验设计以隔离架构固有属性与权重依赖行为。

Result: DLM的高度随机损失景观天然抵抗基于梯度的对抗后缀，但对自然噪声没有保证性防御，说明日常鲁棒性是权重依赖而非架构固有的；DLMs表现出系统性过度自信；机制性探测显示所有模型都能完美编码输入损坏，行为脆弱性完全源于解码器路由失败；表层提示修补无法改善噪声基线。

Conclusion: DLM的鲁棒性无法通过表层补丁实现，必须从根本上融入迭代解码循环之中进行设计。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) offer a compelling alternative to autoregressive (AR) generation by enabling bidirectional context and iterative refinement. However, their reliability under natural input noise and adversarial attacks remains under-explored. To address this, we systematically evaluate DLM robustness and calibration against AR baselines, using two parameter-matched pairs (LLaDA-8B vs. LLaMA-3-8B and Dream-7B vs. Qwen2.5-7B) across 32 natural perturbation conditions, adversarial gradient probes, and mechanistic hidden-state analyses. This paired design effectively isolates architecture-intrinsic properties from weight-dependent behaviors. We find a nuanced robustness profile: while highly stochastic DLM loss landscapes naturally resist gradient-based adversarial suffixes, they provide no guaranteed defense against natural noise, proving that everyday robustness is weight-dependent rather than inherently architectural. Furthermore, DLMs exhibit systematic overconfidence, presenting a practical deployment hazard. Most crucially, mechanistic probing reveals that all models perfectly encode input corruption, isolating behavioral fragility entirely to a decoder routing failure. Consistent with this diagnosis, we show that surface-level prompt patching fails to improve over noisy baselines. Ultimately, DLM robustness cannot be patched on; it must be fundamentally integrated into the iterative decoding loop.

</details>


### [231] [Bridging Inference-Time Scaling and Episodic Memory with Action-Centric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27415)
*Xu Zheng,Chaohao Lin,Zhuomin Chen,Weijieying Ren,Haifeng Chen,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GAMER框架，通过构建动态的"行动中心图"来建模历史推理过程，将记忆机制与LLM解耦，并利用双流时序差分学习对行动节点的正负价值进行估计，从而在推理阶段实现双向决策优化，显著提升推理扩展的效率。




<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理期扩展方法在智能体场景中存在状态无关和冗余搜索的关键低效问题，而现有记忆机制过度依赖LLM的推理能力，导致计算成本过高。

Method: GAMER框架将历史推理建模为动态的行动中心图；将记忆机制与LLM解耦以节省token/成本；采用双流时序差分学习机制，基于过去的成功与失败分别估计行动节点的正（建议）和负（规避）价值；推理阶段通过学习到的价值函数进行双向决策优化，图上的高效搜索提升推理扩展效率。

Result: 在多个基准上，GAMER相比普通基线在成功率上提升20.81%，在进展率上提升6.17%。

Conclusion: GAMER成功弥合了推理扩展与情景记忆之间的差距，通过行动中心图与解耦的记忆机制，以更低的计算成本实现了更优的智能体性能。

Abstract: Recent advancements in inference-time scaling have significantly unlocked the complex reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models~(LLMs). However, for agents, these approaches suffer from a critical inefficiency, operating in a stateless manner and engaging in redundant search processes. Existing memory mechanisms largely rely on the reasoning capabilities of LLMs, leading to prohibitive computational costs. In this paper, we propose a novel framework, \textit{GAMER}~(Graph-based Action-centric Memory with Episodic Reasoning), that bridges the gap between inference scaling and episodic memory. Our approach models historical reasoning as a dynamic \textit{Action-Centric Graph}. By decoupling the memory mechanism from LLMs, our method can save token/money usage by providing less memory context than memory mechanism baselines. To extract knowledge from the graph effectively, we use a dual-stream Temporal Difference learning mechanism to estimate the positive~(suggestion) and negative~(avoidance) value of action nodes based on past successes and failures. During the inference phase, this learned value function optimizes decision-making bi-directionally, so that positive values provide action suggestions, while negative values indicate high-risk actions. By performing efficient searches on the graph, our method significantly improves the efficiency of inference scaling. Experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that \textit{GAMER} achieves superior performance by \textbf{20.81\%/6.17\%} for success/progress rate compared to vanilla baselines.

</details>


### [232] [Dimensionality and Measurement Precision in HLE's Multiple-Choice Subset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27420)
*Mayank Sharma,Savira Nadela,Tyler Matteson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究运用心理测量学方法（IRT模型）对HLE基准的428道题及29个LLM进行评估，发现HLE只测量单一通用推理因子（ωh=0.998），领域标签仅解释3.5%的方差，且测量精度集中在中等能力水平，对最强模型区分度有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HLE作为评估前沿语言模型的广泛使用基准，其八个学科领域的子分数常被解释为不同能力的证据，但尚无研究验证这些标签是否对应经验上可分离的潜在构念，也未评估基准能否有效区分能力相近的模型。

Method: 对29个LLM在HLE文本多选子集（428题）上的表现，拟合双参数逻辑斯蒂克IRT模型，评估基准的维度结构和测量精度分布。具体包括：计算McDonald's ωh检验维度性、分析领域标签对题目反应方差的解释比例、比较领域内与领域间的残差相关、计算领域特定能力估计与总分数的相关性，并分析测试信息函数。

Result: 发现HLE测量单一通用推理因子（ωh=0.998），领域标签仅解释3.5%的项目反应方差，领域内与领域间的残差相关几乎相同（Cohen's d=0.016），领域特定能力估计与总分几乎冗余（r≥0.81）。测试信息函数显示测量精度集中于中等能力水平，在θ>0处急剧下降，而该区域恰是前沿模型所在位置。

Conclusion: HLE的领域子分数不构成独立能力解释的依据，且该基准对最强模型之间的区分能力有限。

Abstract: Humanity's Last Exam (HLE) is widely used to evaluate frontier language models. HLE organizes its questions into eight subject-domain categories, whose subscores are often interpreted as evidence of distinct capabilities. However, no study has assessed whether these labels correspond to empirically separable latent constructs, nor whether the benchmark effectively differentiates between models of similar ability. We evaluate 29 LLMs on the text-only multiple-choice subset of HLE ($J = 428$ items) and apply psychometric methods to assess both the dimensionality of the benchmark and the distribution of its measurement precision. Fitting a two-parameter logistic IRT model, we find convergent evidence that HLE measures a single general reasoning factor: McDonald's $ω_h = 0.998$, domain labels explain only 3.5\% of item response variance, within- and between-domain residual correlations are nearly identical (Cohen's $d = 0.016$), and domain-specific ability estimates are near-redundant with the total score ($r \geq 0.81$). A separate analysis of the test information function reveals that measurement precision concentrates at moderate ability levels and drops sharply above $θ= 0$, where frontier models sit. These findings suggest that HLE's domain subscores do not warrant distinct capability interpretations and that the benchmark's ability to discriminate among the strongest models is limited.

</details>


### [233] [Leveraging Trajectory Graphs for Pre-Execution Error Diagnosis in Agentic LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27443)
*Xu Zheng,Zhuomin Chen,Chaohao Lin,Hua Wei,Haifeng Chen,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Trajectory Graph Copilot框架，通过图调试器（Graph Debugger）将历史轨迹建模为概率图并用图神经网络识别导致失败的序列动作模式，在动作执行前进行早期错误诊断，帮助LLM智能体自我纠错，从而提升其在长时程交互任务中的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长时程交互任务（如具身AI）中面临动作空间庞大、错误累积等问题，一个次优动作就可能导致整个轨迹失败，且有限的步数预算会被浪费在低效或不可恢复的路径上。现有方法需要昂贵微调或无法有效预防错误。

Method: 提出Trajectory Graph Copilot框架：核心组件Graph Debugger将历史轨迹建模为概率图，使用图神经网络识别频繁导致失败的序列动作模式，作为主动式诊断沙箱，在动作执行前提供早期警告，促使智能体自我纠错。

Result: 在四个基准测试上结合三种LLM智能体进行广泛实验，平均通过率（pass ratio）提升14.69%。

Conclusion: 执行前的错误诊断能够有效防止代价高昂的错误，显著增强LLM智能体完成长时程任务的能力，且无需昂贵的微调成本。

Abstract: Large Language Model~(LLM)-based agents have demonstrated exceptional performance across a wide range of complex interactive tasks. However, they often struggle with long-horizon interactive tasks common in domains, such as embodied AI. The complexity and vast action spaces in these settings lead to compounding errors, where a single suboptimal action can derail an entire trajectory, causing the agent to exhaust its limited step budget on inefficient or unrecoverable paths. To overcome this without costly fine-tuning, we draw inspiration from software debugging, where execution logs are analyzed to preemptively catch errors. We propose \textit{Trajectory Graph Copilot}, a novel framework that acts as a ``copilot'' for LLM agents by diagnosing potential action errors before they are executed. At its core,\textit{Graph Debugger} models historical trajectories as a probabilistic graph and uses a Graph Neural Network to identify sequential action patterns that frequently lead to failure. Functioning as a proactive diagnostic sandbox, our method provides early warnings on potentially flawed actions, prompting the agent to self-correct. This pre-action error diagnosis prevents costly mistakes, significantly enhancing the agent's ability to complete long-horizon tasks successfully. The extensive experiments on four benchmarks with three LLM agents demonstrate a $14.69\%$ pass ratio improvement on average.

</details>


### [234] [VAmoS Bench: Voice Agent Simulation Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27453)
*Joshua Meyer,Sahar Shayegan,Ritiz Tambi,Ali Khan,Sun Kim,Victor Shih,Mehdi Jamei,Andi Partovi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了VAmoS Bench（Voice Agent Simulation Bench），一个用于端到端评估完整语音客服智能体系统的新基准。它通过150个金融客服场景（含状态化模拟呼叫者、真实SQL后端和二进制断言评估）来衡量智能体是否真正解决呼叫（即"containment"），弥补了传统基准只测组件质量而忽略整体任务完成度的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音智能体基准主要衡量组件质量（如词错误率、延迟、自然度、话轮转换）和对话属性，但很少有基准评估智能体是否独立正确地处理了电话呼叫。在客服中心场景中，这被称为"containment"——自动化系统无需转接人工即可解决呼叫的比例。此外，某些呼叫的正确结果应该是拒绝或重定向，现有基准未能捕捉这一点。

Method: VAmoS Bench设计了一个状态化的客户支持任务：智能体扮演虚构银行的信用卡支持代表Riley，可执行冻结、取消、更换或激活信用卡操作。基准包含100个场景，每个场景提供一个有私人目标的模拟呼叫者和一个预置的PostgreSQL后端。平台通过场景填充并激活隔离的模拟环境（呼叫者通过音频联系Riley），约三分之一的场景施加对抗性压力。智能体可使用五个执行真实SQL的工具，每个场景定义二进制断言，由评分器基于呼叫者与智能体的完整对话记录（包括工具调用、参数和返回行）进行评估，从而捕捉"声称改卡但未更新数据库"或"数据库操作正确但泄露受保护信息"等错误。

Result: 该基准首个版本聚焦金融服务领域，其评估协议支持不断演进的排行榜：更多语音智能体可在同一版本上被评估，后续版本可扩展任务和场景。

Conclusion: VAmoS Bench填补了语音智能体评估中"端到端任务完成度"这一关键空白，通过状态化模拟、真实数据库后端和完整对话追踪的二进制断言，能够全面捕捉智能体在呼叫处理中的行为和结果正确性。

Abstract: Production voice agents span cascaded, speech-to-speech, and hybrid architectures. Voice-agent benchmarks typically measure component quality and conversational properties such as word error rate, latency, naturalness, and turn-taking. Fewer measure whether the agent handled a phone call correctly on its own. Contact centers refer to this as ``containment'': the share of phone calls the automated system resolves without handing off to a human. On some phone calls the right outcome is refusal or a redirect. To address this gap, we introduce VAmoS Bench, the Voice Agent Simulation Bench. It measures complete voice-agent systems end to end in a stateful customer-support task. The agent is Riley, a credit-card support representative for a fictional bank who can freeze, cancel, replace, or activate a card. Each of 100 scenarios supplies a simulated caller with a private goal and a seeded PostgreSQL backend. The platform uses each scenario to populate and activate an isolated simulation in which the caller reaches Riley over audio; roughly one-third apply adversarial pressure. The agent can use five tools that execute real SQL against the backend. Each scenario also defines binary assertions. A grader evaluates them against the complete trace of what the caller and agent said and what the agent did, including tool invocations, arguments, and returned rows. This catches an agent that claims to have changed a card without updating the database, as well as one that makes the right database change while disclosing protected information. This first benchmark version focuses on financial services. Its evaluation protocol supports an evolving leaderboard: additional voice agents can be evaluated on the same version, while later versions can expand the tasks and scenarios.

</details>


### [235] [Skill Use or Skill Theater? Evaluating the Reasoning Backroom in Skill-Augmented Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27484)
*Jinwei Hu,Yi Qi,Xinmiao Huang,Youcheng Sun,Yi Dong,Xiaowei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BACKTRACE评估框架和BACKROOMBench基准，通过反事实干预测量技能对智能体的因果影响，揭示了技能增强型智能体在"声称使用技能"与实际"依赖技能"之间存在系统性差距（称为"推理后台"问题）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法依赖可见推理或智能体自我归因来判断技能使用情况，但这些信号只反映智能体"看似使用"了什么，无法证明技能是否真正改变了其决策。作者希望揭示技能增强型智能体中陈述的技能使用与实际干预测量的影响力之间的系统性差距。

Method: 提出BACKTRACE评估框架：将每个技能条件答案与匹配的无技能反事实配对，对技能含义、措辞、身份、内容和分配进行干预，并在答案确定后才引出归因。实例化为BACKROOMBench基准，涵盖受控逻辑和竞赛数学、多技能条件、单智能体和多智能体设置以及多种模型家族。

Result: 发现普遍的存在性失败：模型陈述的技能使用保持稳定，但因果依赖和有符号效用存在差异，产生"静默采纳"和"表演性使用"。行为效应更可靠地遵循程序内容而非展示的技能身份，而归因陈述对工件可用性反应强烈。基于直接技能使用声明的观测检测器（文本提及、轨迹相似性、LLM评判）无法识别实际依赖技能的决策。在多智能体系统中，技能影响在来源丢失后仍可存活，而无技能团队仍会提及从未提供的技能和来源。

Conclusion: "推理后台"（Reasoning Backroom）是一个普遍的AI溯源问题，审计它需要干预方法而非单纯的观测方法。

Abstract: Reusable skills are becoming a standard interface for extending language agents with task procedures. Yet evaluators usually infer skill use from visible reasoning or the agent's own attribution. These signals show what the agent appears to use, not whether the skill changed its decision. We ask whether skill-augmented agents exhibit a \textbf{Reasoning Backroom}, a systematic gap between stated skill use and intervention-measured influence.
